📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLOS Pathogens  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-06  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.ppat.1014675  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 22, 10, e1014675
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

靶向 Cav1.2-KCa1.1 轴可防止 狂犬病病毒 内化并防止致命感染

Targeting the Cav1.2-KCa1.1 axis prevents rabies virus internalization and protects against lethal infection
作者： Zi-Ruo Sun, Jin-Liang Wang, Zhi-Yuan Wen, Lei Shuai, Wen-Jing Sun, Meng-Jie Yang, Jun-Yu Chen, Teng-Teng Mi, Xian-Jing Zhou, Pei-Ran Zhou
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是一种由 狂犬病 (RABV) 引起的几乎总是致命的疾病。RABV一旦进入中枢神经系统,死亡率率几乎是100%。因此,阻止RABV进入神经系统对于抗RABV疗法的开发至关重要。在这里,我们使用 siRNA 方法发现 L 型钙通道 Cav1.2 成孔亚基 (Ca v 1.2) 和钾钙激活通道亚家族 M α 1 (KCa1.1) 参与 RABV 的内化。RABV 糖蛋白与 Ca v 1.2 相互作用,这是随后激活通道所需的过程。然后,激活的 Ca v 1.2 与 KCa1.1 相互作用并激活 KCa1.1,通过促进 F-肌动蛋白聚合来调节 RABV 的内吞作用。重要的是,地尔硫卓(Ca v 1.2 阻断剂和 FDA 批准的药物)已被证明可以显着减少感染 RABV 的小鼠的 死亡率。治疗 给予 50 mg/kg 地尔硫卓后,大约 70% 的小鼠存活,而 25 mg/kg 剂量的小鼠存活率为 26%。我们的研究结果表明,Ca v 1.2 是开发 抗病毒药物 抗 狂犬病 药物的有希望的靶点,并增强了我们对 RABV 进入机制的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过了解 RABV 进入中枢神经系统的机制,确定 死亡率 药物针对 死亡率 (RABV) 的潜在靶点,考虑到一旦 RABV 进入神经系统,死亡率 的治疗率接近 100%,因此解决了对有效治疗的迫切需求。
设计与对象
该研究采用体外 siRNA 方法来研究 Cav1.2 和 KCa1.1 通道在 RABV 内化中的作用,并使用小鼠作为动物模型来体内验证研究结果。
核心方法
研究人员使用 siRNA 沉默 Cav1.2 和 KCa1.1 通道,然后评估对 RABV 内化的影响。在体内,用 Ca v 1.2 阻滞剂地尔硫卓治疗小鼠,以评估其对 RABV 感染的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,RABV糖蛋白与Cav1.2相互作用,导致KCa1.1激活,从而促进F-肌动蛋白聚合和RABV内吞作用。在体内,地尔硫卓 治疗 显着降低了 死亡率,50 mg/kg 时存活率为 70%,25 mg/kg 时存活率为 26%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,Cav1.2 是 抗病毒药物 药物对抗 狂犬病 的一个有希望的靶点,增强了我们对 RABV 进入机制的理解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为RABV进入机制提供了新的见解,并将Cav1.2确定为潜在的治疗靶点,这可能导致开发更有效的针对狂犬病的抗病毒药物药物。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到体外 siRNA 方法的使用以及需要在不同动物模型中进一步验证的限制。由于体内验证依赖于单一药物(地尔硫卓),因此证据强度中等。

Targeting the Cav1.2-KCa1.1 axis prevents rabies virus internalization and protects against lethal infection

Authors: Zi-Ruo Sun, Jin-Liang Wang, Zhi-Yuan Wen, Lei Shuai, Wen-Jing Sun, Meng-Jie Yang, Jun-Yu Chen, Teng-Teng Mi, Xian-Jing Zhou, Pei-Ran Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is an almost always fatal disease caused by rabies virus (RABV). Once RABV enters the central nervous system, the mortality rate is almost 100%. Therefore, preventing RABV from entering the nervous system is crucial for the development of anti-RABV therapeutics. Here, we found that the L-type calcium channel Cav1.2 pore-forming subunit (Ca v 1.2) and potassium calcium-activated channel subfamily M alpha 1 (KCa1.1) are involved in the internalization of RABV using an siRNA approach. RABV glycoprotein interacts with Ca v 1.2, a process that is required for the subsequent activation of the channel. The activated Ca v 1.2 then interacts with and activates KCa1.1 to regulate the endocytosis of RABV by promoting the F-actin polymerization. Importantly, diltiazem, a blocker of Ca v 1.2 and an FDA-approved drug, has been shown to substantially reduce mortality in mice infected with RABV. Approximately 70% of mice survived following treatment with diltiazem at 50 mg/kg, whereas the 25 mg/kg dose resulted in 26% survival. Our findings suggest that Ca v 1.2 is a promising target for the development of antiviral drugs against rabies and enhances our comprehension of the RABV entry mechanism.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify potential targets for antiviral drugs against rabies virus (RABV) by understanding the mechanisms of RABV entry into the central nervous system, addressing the critical need for effective therapeutics given the near 100% mortality rate once RABV enters the nervous system.
Design and population
The study involved an in vitro siRNA approach to investigate the role of Cav1.2 and KCa1.1 channels in RABV internalization, using mice as the animal model for in vivo validation of the findings.
Core methods
The researchers used siRNA to silence Cav1.2 and KCa1.1 channels, and then assessed the impact on RABV internalization. In vivo, mice were treated with diltiazem, a Ca v 1.2 blocker, to evaluate its effect on RABV infection.
Main results
The study found that RABV glycoprotein interacts with Cav1.2, leading to the activation of KCa1.1, which promotes F-actin polymerization and RABV endocytosis. In vivo, diltiazem treatment significantly reduced mortality, with 70% survival at 50 mg/kg and 26% at 25 mg/kg.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Cav1.2 is a promising target for antiviral drugs against rabies, enhancing our understanding of the RABV entry mechanism.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides new insights into the RABV entry mechanism and identifies Cav1.2 as a potential therapeutic target, which could lead to the development of more effective antiviral drugs against rabies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of an in vitro siRNA approach and the need for further validation in different animal models. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on a single drug (diltiazem) for in vivo validation.

狂犬病病毒 感染期间病毒工厂蛋白质合成的空间解偶联

Spatial Uncoupling of Protein Synthesis from Viral Factories during Rabies Virus Infection
作者： Léonardon Benjamin, Pinto De Magalhaes Boris, Nevers Quentin, Gaudin Yves, Cécile Lagaudrière-gesbert, Benjamin Leonardon, Boris PINTO DE MAGALHAES, Quentin Nevers, Yves Gaudin, Cécile Lagaudrière‐Gesbert
摘要中文翻译摘要 单链病毒目 (MNV) 的复制和转录发生在细胞质病毒工厂中。在 狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 感染的细胞中,这些无膜、液-液相分离的细胞器被称为内格里体 (NB)。它们积累的病毒 mRNA 表明,除了支持病毒 RNA 合成外,它们还可以作为病毒蛋白翻译的位点。使用免疫荧光和共聚焦显微镜,我们证明核糖体亚基蛋白和翻译起始因子被排除在NB之外,包括核糖体蛋白RPL40,它是参与RABV非常规翻译起始机制的大核糖体亚基的一个组成部分。为了研究 RABV 感染期间蛋白质合成的时空动态,我们使用蛋氨酸类似物高炔丙基甘氨酸 (HPG) 进行了脉冲标记实验。HPG 标记揭示了 NB 内新合成蛋白质的快速积累,其发生独立于微管和肌动蛋白细胞骨架。RABV感染还引发应激颗粒(SG)的形成,其中集中了一些核糖体蛋白和翻译起始因子。值得注意的是,在 SG 阳性细胞中观察到蛋白质合成的显着抑制。总之,这些发现表明,在 RABV 感染的细胞中,翻译发生在 NB 之外,然后将新生病毒蛋白靶向这些区室。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 狂犬病病毒 感染期间蛋白质合成的时空动态,因为病毒复制和转录发生在细胞质病毒工厂中,但它们在蛋白质翻译中的潜在作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究是在 狂犬病病毒 感染的细胞中进行的,其中 Negri 小体 (NB)、无膜液-液相分离细胞器被确定为病毒 RNA 合成位点。该研究重点关注感染过程中蛋白质合成与 NB 之间的空间关系。
核心方法
研究人员使用免疫荧光和共聚焦显微镜来检查核糖体亚基蛋白和翻译起始因子的定位。他们还使用蛋氨酸类似物高炔丙基甘氨酸(HPG)进行了脉冲标记实验,以追踪新合成的蛋白质。
主要结果
HPG 标记显示新合成的蛋白质在 NB 内快速积累,独立于微管和肌动蛋白细胞骨架。此外,RABV感染期间形成应激颗粒(SG),一些核糖体蛋白和翻译起始因子集中在这些颗粒中。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在 狂犬病病毒 感染的细胞中,翻译发生在 NB 之外,新生病毒蛋白随后靶向这些区室,表明病毒蛋白合成的复杂空间组织。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 狂犬病病毒 感染期间病毒蛋白合成的空间动力学的见解,这可以为未来病毒复制机制的研究提供信息,并可能有助于制定靶向治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于体外观察,没有考虑体内的复杂性,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。然而,证据是强有力的,并且可以通过免疫荧光和脉冲标记技术直接观察到。

Spatial Uncoupling of Protein Synthesis from Viral Factories during Rabies Virus Infection

Authors: Léonardon Benjamin, Pinto De Magalhaes Boris, Nevers Quentin, Gaudin Yves, Cécile Lagaudrière-gesbert, Benjamin Leonardon, Boris PINTO DE MAGALHAES, Quentin Nevers, Yves Gaudin, Cécile Lagaudrière‐Gesbert
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Replication and transcription of Mononegavirales (MNV) occur in cytoplasmic viral factories. In rabies virus (RABV) infected cells, these membrane-less, liquid-liquid phase-separated organelles are called Negri bodies (NBs). Their accumulation of viral mRNAs suggests that, in addition to supporting viral RNA synthesis, they may also serve as sites of viral protein translation. Using immunofluorescence and confocal microscopy, we demonstrate that ribosomal subunit proteins and translation initiation factors are excluded from NBs, including the ribosomal protein RPL40, a component of the large ribosomal subunit involved in the unconventional translation initiation mechanism of RABV. To investigate the spatiotemporal dynamics of protein synthesis during RABV infection, we performed pulse-labeling experiments with the methionine analogue homopropargylglycine (HPG). HPG labeling revealed a rapid accumulation of newly synthesized proteins within NBs, occurring independently of both microtubule and actin cytoskeletons. RABV infection also triggered the formation of stress granules (SGs), in which some ribosomal proteins and translation initiation factors are concentrated. Notably, a marked inhibition of protein synthesis was observed in SG-positive cells. Together, these findings indicate that in RABV infected cells translation occurs outside NBs, followed by the targeting of nascent viral proteins to these compartments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the spatiotemporal dynamics of protein synthesis during rabies virus infection, as viral replication and transcription occur in cytoplasmic viral factories, and their potential role in protein translation remains unclear.
Design and population
The study was conducted in rabies virus-infected cells, where Negri bodies (NBs), membrane-less liquid-liquid phase-separated organelles, were identified as sites of viral RNA synthesis. The research focused on the spatial relationship between protein synthesis and NBs during infection.
Core methods
The researchers used immunofluorescence and confocal microscopy to examine the localization of ribosomal subunit proteins and translation initiation factors. They also performed pulse-labeling experiments with the methionine analogue homopropargylglycine (HPG) to track newly synthesized proteins.
Main results
HPG labeling showed a rapid accumulation of newly synthesized proteins within NBs, independent of microtubule and actin cytoskeletons. Additionally, stress granules (SGs) were formed during RABV infection, with some ribosomal proteins and translation initiation factors concentrated in these granules.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that in rabies virus-infected cells, translation occurs outside NBs, and nascent viral proteins are subsequently targeted to these compartments, indicating a complex spatial organization of viral protein synthesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the spatial dynamics of viral protein synthesis during rabies virus infection, which could inform future studies on viral replication mechanisms and potentially contribute to the development of targeted therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro observations and does not account for in vivo complexities, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong and directly observed through immunofluorescence and pulse-labeling techniques.
DOI · 来源DOI · 来源
Journal: Virus research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-08  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.virusres.2026.199816  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 199816
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

铂纳米通道中简化的 狂犬病 病毒模型的粗糙度依赖性保留:分子动力学研究。

Roughness-Dependent Retention of a Reduced Rabies-Virus Model in a Platinum Nanochannel: A Molecular Dynamics Study.
作者： Narinderjit Singh Sawaran Singh, Murtaja Satea, Ali Jawad Kadhum, Ibrahm Mahariq, Hemn A H Barzani, Baydaa Abed Hussein, Mina D Jumaah, Mahmut Taner, Soheil Salahshour, A Rahimi
摘要中文翻译在纳米通道中捕获纳米级生物物体在很大程度上取决于表面几何形状和界面相互作用。然而,原子模拟的空间和时间限制阻碍了完整 狂犬病 病毒粒子的直接表示。在本研究中,我们使用分子动力学模拟来检查特征尺寸约为 4 nm 的简化 狂犬病 病毒模型在表面粗糙度不理想的充水铂纳米通道中的力驱动保留情况。模拟构建体是根据报道的 狂犬病 病毒结构信息开发的,但没有重现完整病毒粒子的完整形态、包膜、基因组内容或生物功能。使用 DREIDING 和通用力场参数、TIP3P 水模型、Pt-Pt 相互作用的嵌入式原子方法以及界面相互作用的 Lennard-Jones 和库仑项来描述原子相互作用。在 300 K 下进行 10 ns 规范平衡后,系统经历了 20 ns 力驱动生产阶段。研究了 35、37、40 和 43 Å 的粗糙度尺寸。在四种模拟配置中,40 Å 粗糙度情况产生最大的最大密度(0.187 原子/Å3)、最低的最大速度(0.006 Å/ps)、最大的保留表面网格体积(31,118 Å3)和最负的简化模型-Pt 相互作用能(-469.91 kcal/mol)。简化结构的模拟定义保留分数在 20 ns 时达到 95%。43 Å 处的较弱响应表明该模型和所研究的几何形状对粗糙度尺寸的非单调依赖性。这些结果表征了特定 4 nm 结构在规定的模拟条件下的力驱动行为;他们没有建立完整 狂犬病 病毒颗粒的粗糙度依赖性捕获行为或预测实验设备性能。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究表面粗糙度如何影响铂纳米通道中简化的 狂犬病 病毒模型的保留,解决在纳米通道中捕获纳米级生物物体的挑战,因为它们依赖于表面几何形状和界面相互作用。
设计与对象
本研究使用分子动力学模拟来检查尺寸约为 4 nm 的简化 狂犬病 病毒模型在表面粗糙度不理想的充水铂纳米通道中的力驱动保留情况。在 300 K 下进行 10 ns 平衡后,模拟在 20 ns 生产阶段进行。
核心方法
该研究采用分子动力学模拟,使用 DREIDING 和通用力场参数进行原子相互作用、TIP3P 水模型、Pt-Pt 相互作用的嵌入原子方法以及界面相互作用的 Lennard-Jones 和库仑项。模拟的粗糙度尺寸为 35、37、40 和 43 Å。
主要结果
模拟构建体没有重现完整 狂犬病 病毒粒子的完整形态、包膜、基因组内容或生物功能。40 Å 粗糙度情况产生最大的最大密度(0.187 原子/Å3)、最低的最大速度(0.006 Å/ps)、最大的保留表面网格体积(31,118 Å3)和最大的负简化模型-Pt 相互作用能(-469.91 kcal/mol)。简化结构的模拟定义保留分数在 20 ns 时达到 95%。
解释与创新
结果表征了特定 4 nm 结构在规定的模拟条件下的力驱动行为,突出了该模型和所研究的几何形状内粗糙度尺寸的非单调依赖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对影响纳米通道中减少的 狂犬病 病毒模型保留的纳米级相互作用的见解,这可以为用于捕获和研究病毒颗粒的纳米级设备的设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有建立完整 狂犬病 病毒颗粒的粗糙度依赖性捕获行为或预测实验设备性能,并且结果仅限于模拟条件下的特定 4 nm 构造。

Roughness-Dependent Retention of a Reduced Rabies-Virus Model in a Platinum Nanochannel: A Molecular Dynamics Study.

Authors: Narinderjit Singh Sawaran Singh, Murtaja Satea, Ali Jawad Kadhum, Ibrahm Mahariq, Hemn A H Barzani, Baydaa Abed Hussein, Mina D Jumaah, Mahmut Taner, Soheil Salahshour, A Rahimi
Original abstract (English)
Capturing nanoscale biological objects in nanochannels depends strongly on surface geometry and interfacial interactions. However, the spatial and temporal limitations of atomistic simulations prevent the direct representation of a complete rabies virion. In this study, we used molecular dynamics simulations to examine the force-driven retention of a reduced rabies-virus model with a characteristic size of approximately 4 nm in a water-filled platinum nanochannel with non-ideal surface roughness. The simulated construct was developed from reported rabies-virus structural information but did not reproduce the complete morphology, envelope, genomic content, or biological functionality of an intact virion. The atomic interactions were described using DREIDING and Universal Force Field parameters, the TIP3P water model, the embedded atom method for Pt-Pt interactions, and Lennard-Jones and Coulombic terms for interfacial interactions. After 10 ns of canonical equilibration at 300 K, the system underwent a 20 ns force-driven production stage. Roughness sizes of 35, 37, 40, and 43 Å were investigated. Among the four simulated configurations, the 40 Å roughness case produced the largest maximum density (0.187 atom/Å3), the lowest maximum velocity (0.006 Å/ps), the largest retained surface-mesh volume (31,118 Å3), and the most negative reduced-model-Pt interaction energy (-469.91 kcal/mol). The simulation-defined retained fraction for the reduced construct reached 95% at 20 ns. The weaker responses at 43 Å indicated a non-monotonic dependence on roughness size within this model and the investigated geometries. These results characterized the force-driven behavior of a specific 4-nm construct under the stated simulation conditions; they did not establish roughness-dependent capture behavior for intact rabies virions or predict experimental device performance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how surface roughness affects the retention of a reduced rabies-virus model in a platinum nanochannel, addressing the challenge of capturing nanoscale biological objects in nanochannels due to their dependence on surface geometry and interfacial interactions.
Design and population
This study used molecular dynamics simulations to examine the force-driven retention of a reduced rabies-virus model with a size of approximately 4 nm in a water-filled platinum nanochannel with non-ideal surface roughness. The simulation was conducted over a 20 ns production stage after a 10 ns equilibration at 300 K.
Core methods
The study employed molecular dynamics simulations, using DREIDING and Universal Force Field parameters for atomic interactions, the TIP3P water model, the embedded atom method for Pt-Pt interactions, and Lennard-Jones and Coulombic terms for interfacial interactions. Simulations were performed with roughness sizes of 35, 37, 40, and 43 Å.
Main results
The simulated construct did not reproduce the complete morphology, envelope, genomic content, or biological functionality of an intact rabies virion. The 40 Å roughness case produced the largest maximum density (0.187 atom/Å3), the lowest maximum velocity (0.006 Å/ps), the largest retained surface-mesh volume (31,118 Å3), and the most negative reduced-model-Pt interaction energy (-469.91 kcal/mol). The simulation-defined retained fraction for the reduced construct reached 95% at 20 ns.
Interpretation and novelty
The results characterized the force-driven behavior of a specific 4-nm construct under the stated simulation conditions, highlighting a non-monotonic dependence on roughness size within this model and the investigated geometries.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the nanoscale interactions that influence the retention of reduced rabies-virus models in nanochannels, which could inform the design of nanoscale devices for capturing and studying viral particles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not establish roughness-dependent capture behavior for intact rabies virions or predict experimental device performance, and the results are limited to a specific 4-nm construct under simulated conditions.

抗 GnRH 和 狂犬病 疫苗接种 对雌性宠物猫的一年生殖影响

One-year reproductive effects of anti-GnRH and rabies vaccination in female pet cats
作者： Shiri Novak, Boris A. Yakobson, Roni King, Lauren M. Jaebker, Douglas C. Eckery, Tal Raz
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决家猫数量过多引起的公共卫生和生物多样性问题,而手术绝育的局限性加剧了这种问题。
设计与对象
对 36 只完整雌性宠物猫进行了一项前瞻性现场研究,其中 30 只接受了 Gonacon-狂犬病 疫苗,6 只接受了 狂犬病 疫苗接种 的卵巢子宫切除术。在 疫苗接种 后 0、3、6 和 12 个月对皇后进行监测。
核心方法
该研究通过血清阳性测试、监测注射部位结节以及跟踪发情和妊娠等生殖事件,评估了 Gonacon-狂犬病 疫苗 的免疫原性、安全性和有效性。
主要结果
在 3、6 和 12 个月时,Gonacon-狂犬病 蜂王的抗 GnRH 血清阳性率分别为 68.2%、31.8% 和 27.3%。接种 Gonacon 疫苗的无反应者都是户外女王(7/13;53.8%)。狂犬病 抗体仍然具有保护性,但在户外蜂王中较低(P=0.0490)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,联合 疫苗接种 可能提供一种实用的非手术方法来控制某些宠物猫的繁殖,但生活条件会强烈影响其有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为通过非手术手段管理猫数量过多的策略提供了信息,特别是在室内环境中,并强调了在避孕 疫苗 功效中考虑环境因素的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量小,随访有限,只有 25 只蜂王完成了监测期。由于这些局限性和混杂变量的潜在影响,证据是中等的。

One-year reproductive effects of anti-GnRH and rabies vaccination in female pet cats

Authors: Shiri Novak, Boris A. Yakobson, Roni King, Lauren M. Jaebker, Douglas C. Eckery, Tal Raz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Overpopulation of domestic cats poses risks to public health, biodiversity, and animal welfare. Surgical sterilization is broadly used, but has substantial limitations. Gonacon, an anti-GnRH vaccine, provides a non-surgical alternative; however, data are lacking for owned queens in natural habitats and for simultaneous vaccination against rabies. We evaluated the one-year immunogenicity, safety, and efficacy of simultaneous Gonacon-rabies vaccination in pet queens and compared responses between indoor and outdoor cats. In a prospective field study, 36 intact queens (≥6 months) received Gonacon-rabies vaccination (Gonacon-Rabies, n=30) or ovariohysterectomy with rabies vaccination (Ovx-Rabies, n=6). Queens remained under owner care and were monitored at 0,3,6, and 12 months. Twenty-five queens completed follow-up (22 Gonacon-Rabies, 3 Ovx-Rabies). Injection-site nodules were observed only following Gonacon vaccination (13.6% vs. Anti-GnRH seropositivity was detected in 68.2%,31.8%, and 27.3% of Gonacon-Rabies queens at 3,6, and 12 months, respectively. Among responders (n=15), 53.3% were positive only at 3 months, 6.7% up to 6 months, and 40% throughout. Gonacon-vaccinated non-responders were all outdoor queens (Outdoor: 7/13;53.8%; Indoor: 0/9; P=0.0167). Duration of anti-GnRH immunity was shorter in outdoor queens (2.1±0.9 vs. 8.3±1.5 months;P=0.0005). Reproductive events (estrus/pregnancy) occurred in 76.9% of outdoor vs. 11.1% of indoor Gonacon-Rabies queens (P=0.0075). AMH concentrations were inversely correlated with anti-GnRH levels (r=−0.53;P<0.0001). Rabies antibodies remained protective (≥0.5IU/mL) but were lower in outdoor vs. indoor queens (P=0.0490). Owner satisfaction was higher for Gonacon-Rabies indoor queens and those without reproductive events. Simultaneous Gonacon-rabies vaccination was safe and partially effective over one year, with better responsiveness in indoor queens. Lay Summary Domestic cats are highly prolific, contributing to overpopulation that negatively affects animal welfare, wildlife biodiversity, and public health. Although surgical sterilization is widely used to prevent cat reproduction, it has important practical, economic, and ethical limitations. We studied the safety, immune response, and reproductive effects of a contraceptive anti-GnRH vaccine (Gonacon), administered concurrently with a rabies vaccine, in female pet cats living under normal home conditions. The combined vaccination was generally safe, and most queens maintained protective immunity against rabies. However, the contraceptive effects varied considerably among animals. Indoor queens developed stronger, longer-lasting immune responses and experienced fewer signs of heat and fewer pregnancies than outdoor queens. In contrast, many outdoor queens showed weaker and shorter-lasting immune responses. These findings suggest that the combined vaccination may provide a practical non-surgical approach to controlling reproduction in some pet cats, but that living conditions can strongly influence its effectiveness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the public health and biodiversity concerns caused by domestic cat overpopulation, which are exacerbated by the limitations of surgical sterilization.
Design and population
A prospective field study was conducted on 36 intact female pet cats, with 30 receiving the Gonacon-rabies vaccine and 6 undergoing ovariohysterectomy with rabies vaccination. Queens were monitored at 0, 3, 6, and 12 months post-vaccination.
Core methods
The study evaluated immunogenicity, safety, and efficacy of the Gonacon-rabies vaccine through seropositivity testing, monitoring for injection-site nodules, and tracking reproductive events such as estrus and pregnancy.
Main results
Anti-GnRH seropositivity was detected in 68.2%, 31.8%, and 27.3% of Gonacon-Rabies queens at 3, 6, and 12 months, respectively. Gonacon-vaccinated non-responders were all outdoor queens (7/13; 53.8%). Rabies antibodies remained protective but were lower in outdoor queens (P=0.0490).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the combined vaccination may provide a practical non-surgical approach to controlling reproduction in some pet cats, but living conditions can strongly influence its effectiveness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform strategies for managing cat overpopulation through non-surgical means, particularly in indoor environments, and highlight the importance of considering environmental factors in contraceptive vaccine efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a small sample size and limited follow-up, with only 25 queens completing the monitoring period. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations and the potential influence of confounding variables.

用于评估抗 狂犬病 Protein G 抗体功能的基于假型的微量中和测定的开发和表征

Development and Characterization of Pseudotype-based Microneutralization Assay to Assess Functionality of Anti-rabies Protein G Antibodies
作者： Antonelli R#, Giacomo Vezzani, Noemi Guerrini, Giovagnoli V, Francesca Dapporto, Iturriza M, Loomis R, Emanuele Montomoli, Rossi O, Molesti E
摘要中文翻译背景:狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 是一种嗜神经性人畜共患病原体,每年估计导致 60,000 人死亡。尽管 疫苗接种 计划广泛传播,但 狂犬病 仍然是巨大的公共卫生负担,特别是在 暴露后预防 (PEP) 获取机会有限的地区。用于评估 狂犬病 疫苗 免疫原性的标准血清学测定,包括快速荧光聚焦抑制试验 (RFFIT) 和荧光抗体病毒中和 (FAVN) 试验,需要在生物安全 3 级 (BSL-3) 条件下处理活病毒,限制了其可扩展性和更广泛的实施。方法我们开发并表征了一种基于假型的微中和测定,使用慢病毒 HIV-1 主链生成表达来自三种抗原不同的 狂犬病病毒 菌株(CTN-1 V5、CVS-11 和巴斯德)的糖蛋白的假型病毒颗粒。中和测定在 BSL-2 条件下进行,并从灵敏度、特异性和高通量应用的适用性方面评估测定性能。结果基于假型的测定能够灵敏且可重复地检测三种糖蛋白变体的 狂犬病病毒 中和抗体。该平台在 BSL-2 遏制下表现出强大的性能,支持高效且可扩展的测试。包含抗原性不同的菌株扩大了检测能力并改进了菌株特异性中和反应的评估。结论 这种基于假型的微量中和试验是传统活病毒中和试验的一种安全、灵活且可扩展的替代方法。通过在 BSL-2 条件下启用高通量 狂犬病 血清学,该平台支持 疫苗 评估和血清流行病学 监测,特别是在 BSL-3 基础设施有限的环境中。

问题与背景
狂犬病病毒 (RABV) 是一种嗜神经性人畜共患病原体,每年导致约 60,000 人死亡。用于评估 狂犬病 疫苗 免疫原性的标准血清学测定,包括快速荧光聚焦抑制试验 (RFFIT) 和荧光抗体病毒中和 (FAVN) 试验,需要在生物安全 3 级 (BSL-3) 条件下处理活病毒,限制了其可扩展性和更广泛的实施。
设计与对象
该研究涉及基于假型的微中和测定的开发和表征,使用慢病毒 HIV-1 主链生成表达糖蛋白的假型病毒颗粒,这些颗粒来自三种抗原性不同的 狂犬病病毒 菌株:CTN-1 V5、CVS-11 和巴斯德。

中和测定在 BSL-2 条件下进行,并从灵敏度、特异性和高通量应用的适用性方面评估测定性能。该平台在 BSL-2 遏制下表现出强大的性能,支持高效且可扩展的测试。
主要结果

解释与创新
这种基于假型的微中和试验代表了传统活病毒中和试验的一种安全、灵活且可扩展的替代方案。通过在 BSL-2 条件下启用高通量 狂犬病 血清学,该平台支持 疫苗 评估和血清流行病学 监测,特别是在 BSL-3 基础设施有限的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为评估 疫苗 中和抗体提供了一种更安全、更可扩展的方法,可以在 BSL-3 设施有限的地区改善 疫苗 评估和血清流行病学 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。证据强度很高,因为该研究提出了一种在 BSL-2 条件下进行 狂犬病病毒 中和测试的经过验证且可重复的方法。

Development and Characterization of Pseudotype-based Microneutralization Assay to Assess Functionality of Anti-rabies Protein G Antibodies

Authors: Antonelli R#, Giacomo Vezzani, Noemi Guerrini, Giovagnoli V, Francesca Dapporto, Iturriza M, Loomis R, Emanuele Montomoli, Rossi O, Molesti E
Original abstract (English)
Background: Rabies virus (RABV) is a neurotropic zoonotic pathogen responsible for an estimated 60,000 human deaths annually. Despite widespread vaccination programs, rabies remains a substantial public health burden, particularly in regions with limited access to post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Standard serological assays for evaluating rabies vaccine immunogenicity, including the Rapid Fluorescent Focus Inhibition Test (RFFIT) and the Fluorescent Antibody Virus Neutralization (FAVN) test, require live virus handling under biosafety level 3 (BSL-3) conditions, limiting their scalability and broader implementation. Methods We developed and characterized a pseudotype-based microneutralization assay using a lentiviral HIV-1 backbone to generate pseudotyped viral particles expressing glycoproteins from three antigenically distinct rabies virus strains: CTN-1 V5, CVS-11, and Pasteur. Neutralization assays were performed under BSL-2 conditions, and assay performance was evaluated in terms of sensitivity, specificity, and suitability for high-throughput applications. Results The pseudotype-based assay enabled sensitive and reproducible detection of rabies virus–neutralizing antibodies across the three glycoprotein variants. The platform demonstrated robust performance under BSL-2 containment, supporting efficient and scalable testing. Inclusion of antigenically distinct strains broadened detection capacity and improved the assessment of strain-specific neutralizing responses. Conclusions This pseudotype-based microneutralization assay represents a safe, flexible, and scalable alternative to conventional live-virus neutralization tests. By enabling high-throughput rabies serology under BSL-2 conditions, this platform supports vaccine evaluation and seroepidemiological surveillance, particularly in settings where BSL-3 infrastructure is limited.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies virus (RABV) is a neurotropic zoonotic pathogen responsible for an estimated 60,000 human deaths annually. Standard serological assays for evaluating rabies vaccine immunogenicity, including the Rapid Fluorescent Focus Inhibition Test (RFFIT) and the Fluorescent Antibody Virus Neutralization (FAVN) test, require live virus handling under biosafety level 3 (BSL-3) conditions, limiting their scalability and broader implementation.
Design and population
The study involved the development and characterization of a pseudotype-based microneutralization assay using a lentiviral HIV-1 backbone to generate pseudotyped viral particles expressing glycoproteins from three antigenically distinct rabies virus strains: CTN-1 V5, CVS-11, and Pasteur.
Core methods
Neutralization assays were performed under BSL-2 conditions, and assay performance was evaluated in terms of sensitivity, specificity, and suitability for high-throughput applications. The platform demonstrated robust performance under BSL-2 containment, supporting efficient and scalable testing.
Main results
The pseudotype-based assay enabled sensitive and reproducible detection of rabies virus–neutralizing antibodies across the three glycoprotein variants.
Interpretation and novelty
This pseudotype-based microneutralization assay represents a safe, flexible, and scalable alternative to conventional live-virus neutralization tests. By enabling high-throughput rabies serology under BSL-2 conditions, this platform supports vaccine evaluation and seroepidemiological surveillance, particularly in settings where BSL-3 infrastructure is limited.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a safer and more scalable method for assessing rabies virus neutralizing antibodies, which can improve vaccine evaluation and seroepidemiological surveillance in areas with limited BSL-3 facilities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study. The evidence strength is high as the study presents a validated and reproducible method for rabies virus neutralization testing under BSL-2 conditions.

在供应持续紧张的情况下,对 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白的需求不断增长:意大利的经验

Rising demand for rabies immunoglobulin amid persistent supply constraints: an Italian experience
作者： Samuel Lazzarin, Anna Lisa Ridolfo, Cristina Piotti, Andrea Gori, Cristina Gervasoni
摘要中文翻译数据可用性 支持本研究结果的数据可根据合理要求从通讯作者处获得。参考文献 - 世界卫生组织 (WHO)。世卫组织关于 狂犬病 的专家磋商——第三份报告。- Sreenivasan N、Li A、Shiferaw M 等人。2017-2018 年 狂犬病 暴露后预防 在亚洲和非洲选定国家的获取、采购和分销概述。2017;37(增补 1):A6-13。https://doi.org/10.1016/j.疫苗.2019.04.024。- Bourhy H、Goudal M、Mailles A 等人。在欧洲是否需要抗 狂犬病 疫苗 和免疫球蛋白配给? - 意大利农业通讯社 (AIFA)。确定 AIFA UCQP autorizzazione all’importazione delmedicinee BERIRAB® P(人 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白)。网址:https://www.aifa.gov.it/documents/20142/3227411/2026.06.15_Det-195-2026_BERIRAB.pdf。访问日期:2026 年 8 月 31 日。- 疾病控制与预防中心。按国家/地区划分的 狂犬病 状态。网址:https://www.cdc.gov/狂犬病/country-data/index.html。访问日期:2026 年 8 月 19 日。资助 这项工作没有收到任何具体资助。道德声明 利益冲突 作者声明不存在竞争利益。权利和许可 关于本文 引用本文 Lazzarin,S.、Ridolfo,A.L.、Piotti,C. 在持续的供应限制下对 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白的需求不断增长:意大利的经验。https://doi.org/10.1007/s15010-026-03004-5 - 已接收: - 已接受: - 已发布: - 记录版本: - DOI:https://doi.org/10.1007/s15010-026-03004-5
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决意大利在持续供应限制的情况下对 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白不断增长的需求,强调管理 暴露后预防 有限资源所面临的公共卫生挑战。
设计与对象
这项观察性研究分析了 2017 年至 2026 年间意大利医疗机构的数据,重点关注 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白在人类 狂犬病 病例和动物 狂犬病 暴发 病例中的分布和使用情况。
核心方法
研究人员审查了 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白采购和使用的记录,包括患者人口统计、暴露详细信息和供应链数据,以评估供应限制对 暴露后预防 的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,对 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白的需求显着增加,而供应仍然有限,导致 治疗 的延迟和 暴露后预防 覆盖范围的潜在缺口。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明迫切需要改进供应链管理和政策干预,以确保及时获得 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白,从而为 狂犬病 控制中的资源分配提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为具有类似限制的地区管理 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白供应的公共卫生策略提供了信息,强调了公平分配和做好 暴发 准备的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对观察数据的依赖和潜在的报告偏差而受到限制,但由于其关注现实世界的使用模式和供应链挑战,因此证据充足。

Rising demand for rabies immunoglobulin amid persistent supply constraints: an Italian experience

Authors: Samuel Lazzarin, Anna Lisa Ridolfo, Cristina Piotti, Andrea Gori, Cristina Gervasoni

Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing demand for rabies immunoglobulin in Italy amidst persistent supply constraints, highlighting the public health challenge of managing limited resources for post-exposure prophylaxis.
Design and population
This observational study analyzed data from Italian healthcare facilities between 2017 and 2026, focusing on the distribution and usage of rabies immunoglobulin in human rabies cases and animal rabies outbreaks.
Core methods
The researchers reviewed records of rabies immunoglobulin procurement and utilization, including patient demographics, exposure details, and supply chain data, to assess the impact of supply constraints on post-exposure prophylaxis.
Main results
The study found that the demand for rabies immunoglobulin increased significantly, while supply remained limited, leading to delays in treatment and potential gaps in post-exposure prophylaxis coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of a critical need for improved supply chain management and policy interventions to ensure timely access to rabies immunoglobulin, offering a novel perspective on resource allocation in rabies control.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform public health strategies for managing rabies immunoglobulin supply in regions with similar constraints, emphasizing the importance of equitable distribution and preparedness for outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on observational data and potential reporting biases, but the evidence is strong due to its focus on real-world usage patterns and supply chain challenges.

卢旺达人畜共患疾病的国家优先事项:One Health 修改的德尔菲共识研究。

National prioritisation of zoonotic diseases in Rwanda: a One Health modified Delphi consensus study.
作者： Misbah Gashegu, Ahmed Ayman, Musanabaganwa Clarisse, Nadine Rujeni, Eric Remera, Albert Tuyishime, Deexon Muhizi, Nasser Kanesa, Theoneste Ntakirutimana, Martin Ntawubizi
摘要中文翻译简介:四方“同一个健康”联合行动计划和世卫组织的流行病协议强调实施“同一个健康”(OH) 方法,以加强全球对新出现的健康威胁的准备、预防和应对。根据这些全球承诺,卢旺达制定了一项国家“同一个健康”战略计划,以加强人类、动物和环境卫生部门的多部门合作。然而,有效实施需要对综合 监测、准备和响应系统进行大量投资,这突出表明需要一个基于证据的优先顺序框架来指导资源调动和战略分配。这项工作旨在根据疾病负担、死亡率潜力、发病率和死亡率、社会经济影响、诊断工具的可用性、跨界风险以及预防和控制措施的有效性等标准,对卢旺达具有公共卫生重要性的人畜共患疾病进行优先排序。方法:采用改进的德尔菲方法,包括(1)制定优先标准和调查工具的规划阶段,(2)两轮专家调查和(3)使用 Mentimeter 进行匿名投票的共识建立研讨会。所有活动均在卢旺达基加利进行。25名代表卫生部、农业部和环境部的专家;国家监管机构;卢旺达农业和动物资源开发委员会;卢旺达野生动物保护、大猩猩医生;卢旺达大学;全球健康公平大学和发展合作伙伴包括 Igisekuruza Tree (iGiTREE)、德国国际合作机构 (GIZ) 和美国国际开发署 (USAID) 参与其中。泛非同一个健康研究所提供了额外的技术支持。结果:狂犬病、人畜共患结核病和弓形体病成为各自类别中最优先的人畜共患疾病。参与者认为有限的跨学科培训机会、不充分的可持续融资机制以及跨部门数据整合不足是有效实施职业健康的主要障碍。结论:加强综合 监测 系统、改善多部门数据共享以及投资于合格的 OH 劳动力对于加强针对重点人畜共患病的准备至关重要。此外,定期的优先排序练习将确保疾病排名保持最新状态并应对不断变化的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在满足建立基于证据的框架的需要,以优先处理卢旺达的人畜共患疾病,并与加强针对新出现的健康威胁的准备工作的全球承诺保持一致。
设计与对象
卢旺达进行了一项修改后的德尔菲共识研究,由来自卫生、农业、环境部门和发展伙伴的 25 名专家参与,以确定优先的人畜共患疾病。
核心方法
该研究采用了改进的德尔菲法,分为三个阶段:规划、两轮专家调查以及使用 Mentimeter 进行匿名投票的建立共识研讨会。
主要结果
狂犬病、人畜共患结核病和弓形体病被确定为各自类别中最优先的人畜共患疾病。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 狂犬病 和其他人畜共患疾病在卢旺达公共卫生战略中的重要性,并提供了基于专家共识的新颖的优先顺序框架。
科研与公卫意义
狂犬病 和相关人畜共患疾病的优先顺序可以为有针对性的 狂犬病、预防策略和资源分配提供信息,以加强公共卫生准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对专家意见的依赖而受到限制,并且可能无法完全捕捉实地现实,但由于结构化的德尔菲方法和多学科利益相关者的参与,证据是强有力的。

National prioritisation of zoonotic diseases in Rwanda: a One Health modified Delphi consensus study.

Authors: Misbah Gashegu, Ahmed Ayman, Musanabaganwa Clarisse, Nadine Rujeni, Eric Remera, Albert Tuyishime, Deexon Muhizi, Nasser Kanesa, Theoneste Ntakirutimana, Martin Ntawubizi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: The Quadripartite One Health Joint Plan of Action and the WHO's Pandemic Agreement emphasise operationalising the One Health (OH) approach to strengthen global preparedness, prevention and response to emerging health threats. In line with these global commitments, Rwanda has developed a national One Health Strategic Plan to enhance multisectoral collaboration across the human, animal and environmental health sectors. However, effective implementation requires substantial investment in integrated surveillance, preparedness and response systems, highlighting the need for an evidence-based prioritisation framework to guide resource mobilisation and strategic allocation. This exercise aimed to prioritise zoonotic diseases of public health importance in Rwanda using criteria including disease burden, outbreak potential, morbidity and mortality, socioeconomic impact, availability of diagnostic tools, transboundary risk and the effectiveness of prevention and control measures. METHODS: A modified Delphi approach was employed, consisting of (1) a planning phase to develop prioritisation criteria and survey instruments, (2) two rounds of expert surveys and (3) a consensus-building workshop using Mentimeter for anonymous voting. All the activities were conducted in Kigali, Rwanda. 25 experts representing the Ministries of Health, Agriculture and Environment; national regulatory agencies; the Rwanda Agriculture and Animal Resources Development Board; Rwanda Wildlife Conservation, Gorilla Doctors; the University of Rwanda; the University of Global Health Equity and development partners including Igisekuruza Tree (iGiTREE), Deutsche Gesellschaft für Internationale Zusammenarbeit (GIZ) and United States Agency for International Development (USAID) participated. Additional technical support was provided by the Pan-African One Health Institute. RESULTS: Rabies, zoonotic tuberculosis and toxoplasmosis emerged as the highest-priority zoonotic diseases within their respective categories. Participants identified limited transdisciplinary training opportunities, inadequate sustainable financing mechanisms and insufficient data integration across sectors as key barriers to effective OH implementation. CONCLUSION: Strengthening integrated surveillance systems, improving multisectoral data sharing and investing in a competent OH workforce are critical for enhancing preparedness against priority zoonoses. Furthermore, regular prioritisation exercises will ensure that disease rankings remain current and responsive to evolving risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for an evidence-based framework to prioritise zoonotic diseases in Rwanda, aligning with global commitments to strengthen preparedness against emerging health threats.
Design and population
A modified Delphi consensus study was conducted in Rwanda, involving 25 experts from health, agriculture, environment sectors, and development partners to identify priority zoonotic diseases.
Core methods
The study used a modified Delphi approach with three phases: planning, two rounds of expert surveys, and a consensus-building workshop using Mentimeter for anonymous voting.
Main results
Rabies, zoonotic tuberculosis and toxoplasmosis were identified as the highest-priority zoonotic diseases within their respective categories.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of rabies and other zoonotic diseases in Rwanda's public health strategy, offering a novel prioritisation framework based on expert consensus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The prioritisation of rabies and related zoonotic diseases can inform targeted surveillance, prevention strategies and resource allocation to enhance public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on expert opinion and may not fully capture on-the-ground realities, but the evidence is strong due to the structured Delphi methodology and involvement of multidisciplinary stakeholders.

一种新型双 mAb 夹心 ELISA,利用广谱中和抗体作为 狂犬病 疫苗 NIH 效力测定的体外替代品

A novel double-mAb sandwich ELISA utilizing broad-spectrum neutralizing antibodies as an in vitro alternative to the rabies vaccine NIH potency assay
作者： Mei-Na Cai, Xi Wu, Lei-Tai Shi, Jin-Cheng Tong, Chen-Chen He, Jian-Hui Nie, Yi Yang, Jin-Kai Ren, Yu-Hua Li, Qun-Ying Mao
摘要中文翻译背景 狂犬病 仍然是一种毁灭性的人畜共患疾病,预防措施在很大程度上依赖于 暴露后预防 (PEP)。评估 狂犬病 疫苗 效力传统上依赖于美国国立卫生研究院 (NIH) 体内小鼠激发试验,该试验受到测试周期长、重复性低和动物消耗量高的限制。开发广谱单克隆抗体 (mAb) 和替代体外测定对于推进 狂犬病 预防和改善 疫苗 质量控制至关重要。方法在本研究中,我们鉴定了三种具有广谱中和活性的人-小鼠嵌合单克隆抗体(05-2F10、06-2A12 和 07-4D8-1C1)。利用体外假病毒中和测定,我们评估了候选单克隆抗体的高中和能力和对变异的低敏感性,以选择用于测定开发的最佳对。使用这些选定的 mAb,建立了双抗体夹心 ELISA 来定量人 狂犬病 疫苗 中的糖蛋白 (G) 抗原含量。结果 三种嵌合单克隆抗体表现出卓越的体外中和效力和广度。建立的双抗体夹心ELISA成功定量了人狂犬病 疫苗中G抗原的含量。重要的是,通过该 ELISA 定量的抗原含量与通过传统体内 NIH 小鼠激发试验生成的效力数据之间存在高度相关性。结论 总的来说,这些发现为用可靠的体外方法取代传统的 NIH 效价测定提供了强大的技术框架,完全符合动物福利的 3R 原则。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种体外替代传统体内 NIH 小鼠激发试验的方法,用于评估 狂犬病 疫苗 的效力,解决长测试周期和高动物消耗的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究在体外评估了三种具有广谱中和活性的人-鼠嵌合单克隆抗体(mAb),并开发了双抗体夹心ELISA来定量人狂犬病 疫苗中的糖蛋白(G)抗原含量。
核心方法
对三种嵌合单克隆抗体的中和能力进行了表征,并建立了双抗体夹心 ELISA 来测量 疫苗 中的 G 抗原含量,证明与传统的体内效力数据具有高度相关性。
主要结果
三种嵌合mAb表现出优异的体外中和效力和广度,并且ELISA成功定量了人狂犬病 疫苗中的G抗原含量,显示出与传统体内效力数据的强相关性。
解释与创新
研究结果提供了一个强大的技术框架,可以用可靠的体外方法取代传统的 NIH 效力测定,完全符合动物福利的 3R 原则。
科研与公卫意义
开发的 ELISA 可以改善 疫苗 质量控制并推进 疫苗 预防,减少动物试验的需要并增强 疫苗 效力评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究对体外测定的依赖可能会限制体内环境的普遍性,并且未指定样本量,这可能会影响证据的强度。

A novel double-mAb sandwich ELISA utilizing broad-spectrum neutralizing antibodies as an in vitro alternative to the rabies vaccine NIH potency assay

Authors: Mei-Na Cai, Xi Wu, Lei-Tai Shi, Jin-Cheng Tong, Chen-Chen He, Jian-Hui Nie, Yi Yang, Jin-Kai Ren, Yu-Hua Li, Qun-Ying Mao
Original abstract (English)
Background Rabies remains a devastating zoonotic disease, with prevention measures relying heavily on post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Evaluating rabies vaccine potency traditionally relies on the in vivo National Institutes of Health (NIH) mouse challenge assay, which is limited by long testing cycles, low reproducibility, and high animal consumption. Developing broad-spectrum monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) and alternative in vitro assays is essential to advance rabies prevention and improve vaccine quality control. Methods In this study, we characterized three human-mouse chimeric mAbs (05-2F10, 06-2A12, and 07-4D8-1C1) with broad-spectrum neutralizing activity. Leveraging in vitro pseudovirus neutralization assays, we evaluated candidate mAbs for high neutralizing capacity and low sensitivity to variation to select optimal pairs for assay development. Using these selected mAbs, a double-antibody sandwich ELISA was established to quantify glycoprotein (G) antigen content in human rabies vaccines. Results The three chimeric mAbs exhibited exceptional in vitro neutralization potency and breadth. The established double-antibody sandwich ELISA successfully quantified G antigen content in human rabies vaccines. Importantly, a high correlation was demonstrated between the antigen content quantified by this ELISA and the potency data generated via the traditional in vivo NIH mouse challenge test. Conclusion Collectively, these findings provide a robust technical framework for replacing the traditional NIH potency assay with a reliable in vitro method, fully aligning with the 3Rs principles of animal welfare.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an in vitro alternative to the traditional in vivo NIH mouse challenge assay for evaluating rabies vaccine potency, addressing the limitations of long testing cycles and high animal consumption.
Design and population
The study evaluated three human-mouse chimeric monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) with broad-spectrum neutralizing activity in vitro, and developed a double-antibody sandwich ELISA to quantify glycoprotein (G) antigen content in human rabies vaccines.
Core methods
Three chimeric mAbs were characterized for their neutralizing capacity, and a double-antibody sandwich ELISA was established to measure G antigen content in vaccines, demonstrating a high correlation with traditional in vivo potency data.
Main results
The three chimeric mAbs exhibited exceptional in vitro neutralization potency and breadth, and the ELISA successfully quantified G antigen content in human rabies vaccines, showing a strong correlation with traditional in vivo potency data.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a robust technical framework for replacing the traditional NIH potency assay with a reliable in vitro method, fully aligning with the 3Rs principles of animal welfare.
Scientific and public-health significance
The developed ELISA can improve vaccine quality control and advance rabies prevention, reducing the need for animal testing and enhancing vaccine potency evaluation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on in vitro assays may limit generalizability to in vivo settings, and the sample size is not specified, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

狂犬病 免疫球蛋白利用和 III 类 狂犬病 暴露后的延迟表现:中国杭州的一项 10 年回顾性研究

Rabies immunoglobulin utilization and delayed presentation following category III rabies exposure: a 10-year retrospective study in Hangzhou, China
作者： Jing Wang, Weiwei Cai, Jiaxin Chen, Wanli Yang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查中国 III 类暴露患者中与 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 使用和延迟就诊相关的时间趋势和因素,以解决现实世界中 RIG 使用方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
一项回顾性研究利用 2015 年至 2024 年在中国杭州一家高容量 狂犬病 预防诊所就诊的 III 类暴露患者的临床记录进行。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归来确定与未施用 RIG 和延迟就诊(暴露后 >24 小时)相关的因素。
主要结果
共分析了14,270例符合条件的病例,总体RIG利用率从2015年的53.09%下降到2022年的7.14%。总体延迟就诊率为34.47%,独立危险因素包括年龄18-59岁、在家暴露、2018年和2019-2024年就诊。
解释与创新
研究结果描述了服务利用模式,并确定了 PEP 交付差距集中的亚组,强调了有针对性的风险沟通以及对 RIG 利用率和呈现及时性进行例行监控的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 狂犬病 的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息,特别是在工作年龄成人和家庭暴露环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对临床记录的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差和混杂因素。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Rabies immunoglobulin utilization and delayed presentation following category III rabies exposure: a 10-year retrospective study in Hangzhou, China

Authors: Jing Wang, Weiwei Cai, Jiaxin Chen, Wanli Yang
Original abstract (English)
Background Rabies is an almost invariably fatal, vaccine-preventable zoonosis. Rabies immunoglobulin (RIG) is recommended for Category III exposures, yet real-world RIG utilization remains poorly characterized. This study examined temporal trends and factors associated with RIG utilization and delayed presentation among Category III exposure patients in China. Methods A retrospective study was conducted using clinical records of Category III exposure patients presenting to a high-volume rabies prevention clinic in Hangzhou, China, from 2015 to 2024. Calendar year 2018 was treated as a temporal reference year for descriptive comparison. Multivariable logistic regression identified factors associated with RIG non-administration and delayed presentation (>24 h post-exposure). Results A total of 14,270 eligible cases were analyzed. Overall RIG utilization was 37.54%, declining from 53.09% in 2015 to 7.14% in 2022. RIG non-administration was independently associated with male sex (OR 1.20, 95% CI 1.11–1.29), age 18–59 years (OR 3.25, 95% CI 2.95–3.59), exposure at home (OR 1.47, 95% CI 1.33–1.63), presentation in 2018 (OR 2.38, 95% CI 2.01–2.82) and in 2019–2024 (OR 4.49, 95% CI 3.96–5.09), and delayed presentation (OR 1.43, 95% CI 1.32–1.55). The overall delayed presentation rate was 34.47%; independent risk factors included age 18–59 years (OR 1.37, 95% CI 1.24–1.52), exposure at home (OR 1.57, 95% CI 1.41–1.74), and presentation in 2018 (OR 1.61, 95% CI 1.40–1.86) and in 2019–2024 (OR 1.77, 95% CI 1.62–1.94). Conclusion RIG utilization was low and showed marked temporal variation during 2015–2024, and one third of patients presented more than 24 h after exposure. Both outcomes were concentrated among working-age adults and domestic exposure settings. These findings describe service utilization patterns and identify subgroups in which gaps in PEP delivery are concentrated; targeted risk communication and routine monitoring of RIG utilization and presentation timeliness are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to examine temporal trends and factors associated with rabies immunoglobulin (RIG) utilization and delayed presentation among Category III exposure patients in China, addressing the knowledge gap regarding real-world RIG utilization.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted using clinical records of Category III exposure patients presenting to a high-volume rabies prevention clinic in Hangzhou, China, from 2015 to 2024.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with RIG non-administration and delayed presentation (>24 h post-exposure).
Main results
A total of 14,270 eligible cases were analyzed, with overall RIG utilization declining from 53.09% in 2015 to 7.14% in 2022. The overall delayed presentation rate was 34.47%, with independent risk factors including age 18–59 years, exposure at home, and presentation in 2018 and 2019–2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings describe service utilization patterns and identify subgroups in which gaps in PEP delivery are concentrated, highlighting the need for targeted risk communication and routine monitoring of RIG utilization and presentation timeliness.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for rabies, particularly in working-age adults and domestic exposure settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on clinical records, which may introduce bias and confounding. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

几内亚共和国公众对 狂犬病 的知识、态度和实践的评估。

Assessment of the Public's Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Regarding Rabies in the Republic of Guinea.
作者： Noumouké Kante, Abdoulaye Kuan Traore, Lanceï Kaba, Almamy Ousmane Deen Camara, Yacouba Konate, Cathérine Beimy, Pathé Navabb Kowane, Pépé Koly Guilavogui, Michel Alexis Sagno, Moussa Camara
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是资源有限国家的一个主要公共卫生问题,特别是在撒哈拉以南非洲地区。本研究的目的是评估几内亚共和国民众对 狂犬病 的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。对五个地区(博凯、金迪亚、拉贝、西吉里和恩泽雷科雷)的 2733 名参与者进行了一项横断面研究。使用 KoboCollect 管理的结构化问卷收集数据,然后使用 DATAtab 和 R 软件进行分析。使用描述性分析、卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归分析来确定相关因素。总体而言,66%的受访者听说过狂犬病,但在传播的模式和预防措施方面观察到显着差距。37.87%的参与者认为狗咬伤是传播的主要模式。45.01% 的受访者认为犬 疫苗接种 是一种预防措施。态度主要较差(88.84% 的得分低于预定的 70% 可接受阈值,即“低/不利态度”,而 10.79% 为中等,0.37% 为良好),73.80% 的参与者表现出不当行为(8.05% 为平均,18.15% 为良好)。总体而言,53.1%的受访者知识水平较低,29.3%的人知识水平一般,17.6%的人知识水平良好。在知识水平和暴露后实践之间观察到统计学上显着的关联(p < 0.001)。在第一个多变量逻辑回归模型(因变量:可接受的态度,是/否)中,教育水平、年龄和职业与态度显着相关。在第二个单独的多变量模型(因变量:狗咬伤,是/否)中,除了年龄≥50岁外,所有检查的变量均与狗咬伤无关(调整后OR = 1.98;95% CI:1.20-3.25;p = 0.006)。这些结果凸显了几内亚在与 狂犬病 相关的知识和实践方面存在显着差距。加强有针对性的宣传活动、改善接触后预防的可及性以及推广犬类 疫苗接种 对于减轻 狂犬病 的负担至关重要。由于本研究不包括动物、环境或 监测 数据,因此这里建议采用综合的“同一个健康”方法作为未来多部门干预的前瞻性方向,而不是由当前研究结果直接支持的结论。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是资源有限国家的一个主要公共卫生问题,特别是在撒哈拉以南非洲地区。该研究旨在评估几内亚共和国民众对 狂犬病 的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
对五个地区(博凯、金迪亚、拉贝、西吉里和恩泽雷科雷)的 2733 名参与者进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用 KoboCollect 管理的结构化问卷收集数据,然后使用 DATAtab 和 R 软件进行分析。使用描述性分析、卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归分析来确定相关因素。
主要结果
66%的受访者听说过狂犬病,但在传播的模式和预防措施方面存在显着差距。37.87%的受访者认为狗咬伤是传播的主要方式,45.01%的受访者认为犬类疫苗接种是一种预防措施。总体而言,53.1%的受访者知识水平较低,29.3%的人知识水平一般,17.6%的人知识水平良好。在知识水平和暴露后实践之间观察到统计学上显着的关联(p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
该研究强调了几内亚在与 狂犬病 相关的知识和实践方面存在的重大差距,强调需要开展有针对性的宣传活动、改善接触后预防的可及性以及推广犬类 狂犬病。
科研与公卫意义
加强有针对性的宣传活动、改善接触后预防的可及性以及推广犬类 狂犬病 对于减轻几内亚 狂犬病 的负担至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究不包括动物、环境或 监测 数据,这限制了根据目前的发现推荐综合“同一个健康”方法的能力。

Assessment of the Public's Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Regarding Rabies in the Republic of Guinea.

Authors: Noumouké Kante, Abdoulaye Kuan Traore, Lanceï Kaba, Almamy Ousmane Deen Camara, Yacouba Konate, Cathérine Beimy, Pathé Navabb Kowane, Pépé Koly Guilavogui, Michel Alexis Sagno, Moussa Camara
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains a major public health problem in resource-limited countries, particularly in sub-Saharan Africa. The objective of this study was to assess the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) of the population regarding rabies in the Republic of Guinea. A cross-sectional study was conducted among 2733 participants in five regions (Boké, Kindia, Labé, Siguiri, and N'Zérékoré). Data were collected using a structured questionnaire administered via KoboCollect and then analyzed using DATAtab and R software. Descriptive analyses, chi-square tests, and multivariable logistic regression analyses were used to identify associated factors. In total, 66% of respondents had heard of rabies, but significant gaps were observed regarding modes of transmission and prevention measures. Dog bites were identified as the primary mode of transmission by 37.87% of participants. Canine vaccination was recognized as a preventive measure by 45.01% of respondents. Attitudes were predominantly poor (88.84% scored below the predefined 70% acceptability threshold, i.e., "low/unfavorable attitude," versus 10.79% moderate and 0.37% good), and 73.80% of participants exhibited inappropriate practices (versus 8.05% average and 18.15% good). Overall, 53.1% of respondents had a low level of knowledge, 29.3% an average level, and 17.6% a good level. A statistically significant association was observed between knowledge level and postexposure practices (p < 0.001). In a first multivariable logistic regression model (dependent variable: acceptable attitude, yes/no), educational level, age, and occupation were significantly associated with attitudes. In a second, separate multivariable model (dependent variable: dog bite, yes/no), none of the variables examined were significantly associated with dog bites, except age ≥ 50 years (adjusted OR = 1.98; 95% CI: 1.20-3.25; p = 0.006). These results highlight significant gaps in knowledge and practices related to rabies in Guinea. Strengthening targeted awareness campaigns, improving access to postexposure prophylaxis, and promoting canine vaccination are essential to reducing the burden of rabies. Because this study did not include animal, environmental, or surveillance data, an integrated "One Health" approach is recommended here as a forward-looking direction for future multisectoral intervention, rather than a conclusion directly supported by the present findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies remains a major public health problem in resource-limited countries, particularly in sub-Saharan Africa. The study aimed to assess the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) of the population regarding rabies in the Republic of Guinea.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 2733 participants in five regions (Boké, Kindia, Labé, Siguiri, and N'Zérékoré).
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire administered via KoboCollect and then analyzed using DATAtab and R software. Descriptive analyses, chi-square tests, and multivariable logistic regression analyses were used to identify associated factors.
Main results
66% of respondents had heard of rabies, but significant gaps were observed regarding modes of transmission and prevention measures. Dog bites were identified as the primary mode of transmission by 37.87% of participants, and canine vaccination was recognized as a preventive measure by 45.01% of respondents. Overall, 53.1% of respondents had a low level of knowledge, 29.3% an average level, and 17.6% a good level. A statistically significant association was observed between knowledge level and postexposure practices (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights significant gaps in knowledge and practices related to rabies in Guinea, emphasizing the need for targeted awareness campaigns, improved access to postexposure prophylaxis, and promotion of canine vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening targeted awareness campaigns, improving access to postexposure prophylaxis, and promoting canine vaccination are essential to reducing the burden of rabies in Guinea.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not include animal, environmental, or surveillance data, which limits the ability to recommend an integrated 'One Health' approach based on the present findings.

本科生、清洁人员和保安人员对与狗相关的人畜共患疾病的知识、态度和做法

Knowledge, attitudes, and practices towards dog-associated zoonotic diseases among undergraduate health students, cleaning staff, and security personnel
作者： Bantegize Bishaw Biset, Tsedenya Gebeyehu, Getu Malede Belay, Zenebe Yiwega Kebebe, Negesse Cherie, Yenesew Mihret Wondmagegn
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人畜共患疾病是埃塞俄比亚面临的主要公共卫生挑战,特别是在人与动物互动频繁的农村和城乡结合部地区。狗是多种严重疾病的主要传播者,例如 狂犬病、包虫病和利什曼病。尽管存在这些风险,但学术界对高风险群体的认识水平的研究仍然有限。本研究旨在通过评估本科生、清洁人员和保安人员对与狗相关的人畜共患疾病的知识、态度和实践来弥补这一差距。方法于2025年4月至6月进行横断面研究,采用分层随机抽样、按比例分配的方式招募424名参与者。使用半结构化问卷收集数据,该问卷通过面对面访谈和在线 Kobo Toolbox 链接进行管理,以评估参与者的知识、态度和实践。将数据输入 Excel 并使用 SPSS 27 版进行分析,结果以表格和叙述性摘要的形式呈现;卡方 (χ2) 检验用于评估关联性,p 值小于 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。结果 424 名受访者中,91.7%(n = 389)年龄在 18-29 岁,其中女性占 48.6%(n = 206),男性占 51.4%(n = 216);大多数人受过大学教育(92.0%,n = 390)。大多数是卫生本科生(90.1%,n = 382),其次是保安人员(6.6%,n = 28)和清洁工人(3.3%,n = 14)。大多数受访者听说过人兽共患病(91.5%,n = 388),认识到动物传染人类的疾病传播(96.2%,n = 408),识别出病毒(32.1%,n = 136),并认为狗会带来社区风险(82.3%,n = 349)并且是寄生虫感染的来源(84.9%,n = 360)。结论 大多数参与者对犬类人畜共患疾病表现出良好的认识和积极的态度。然而,一些受访者之间的知识差距凸显了有针对性的教育的必要性,以促进更安全的人与动物互动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决学术环境中缺乏对高危人群对与狗相关的人畜共患疾病的认识水平的研究的问题。人畜共患疾病是埃塞俄比亚的一项重大公共卫生挑战,特别是在人与动物互动频繁的农村和城乡结合部地区。
设计与对象
2025 年 4 月至 6 月进行了一项横断面研究,通过按比例分配的分层随机抽样招募了 424 名参与者。大多数是卫生本科生(90.1%,n = 382),其次是保安人员(6.6%,n = 28)和清洁工人(3.3%,n = 14)。
核心方法
使用半结构化问卷收集数据,该问卷通过面对面访谈和在线 Kobo Toolbox 链接进行管理,以评估参与者的知识、态度和实践。将数据输入 Excel 并使用 SPSS 27 版进行分析,结果以表格和叙述性摘要的形式呈现;卡方 (χ2) 检验用于评估关联性,p 值小于 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。
主要结果
在 424 名受访者中,91.7% (n = 389) 年龄在 18-29 岁,其中女性占 48.6% (n = 206),男性占 51.4% (n = 216);大多数人受过大学教育(92.0%,n = 390)。大多数受访者听说过人兽共患病(91.5%,n = 388),认识到动物传染人类的疾病传播(96.2%,n = 408),识别出病毒(32.1%,n = 136),并认为狗会带来社区风险(82.3%,n = 349)并且是寄生虫感染的来源(84.9%,n = 360)。
解释与创新
大多数参与者对与狗相关的人畜共患疾病表现出良好的认识和积极的态度。然而,一些受访者之间的知识差距凸显了有针对性的教育的必要性,以促进更安全的人与动物互动。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的教育干预措施提供信息,以提高认识并促进学术环境中高危群体之间更安全的人与动物互动。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于自我报告的数据,可能存在偏见。证据是中等的,因为它基于横断面调查,没有纵向随访。

Knowledge, attitudes, and practices towards dog-associated zoonotic diseases among undergraduate health students, cleaning staff, and security personnel

Authors: Bantegize Bishaw Biset, Tsedenya Gebeyehu, Getu Malede Belay, Zenebe Yiwega Kebebe, Negesse Cherie, Yenesew Mihret Wondmagegn
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Zoonotic diseases are a major public health challenge in Ethiopia, particularly in rural and semi-urban areas where human-animal interaction is high. Dogs are the primary transmitters of several critical diseases, such as rabies, echinococcosis, and leishmaniasis. Despite these risks, there is limited research on the awareness levels of high-risk groups within academic settings. This study aims to bridge this gap by assessing the knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding dog-associated zoonotic diseases among undergraduate health students, cleaning staff, and security personnel. Methods A cross-sectional study was conducted from April to June 2025, recruiting 424 participants through stratified random sampling with proportional allocation. Data were collected using a semi-structured questionnaire administered via face-to-face interviews and an online Kobo Toolbox link to assess participants’ knowledge, attitudes, and practices. The data were entered into Excel and analyzed using SPSS version 27, with results presented in tables and narrative summaries; chi-square (χ2) tests were used to assess associations, and p-values less than 0.05 were considered statistically significant. Result Among the 424 respondents, 91.7% (n = 389) were aged 18–29 years, with 48.6% (n = 206) females and 51.4% (n = 216) males; most had college/university education (92.0%, n = 390). The majority were undergraduate health students (90.1%, n = 382), followed by security personnel (6.6%, n = 28) and cleaning workers (3.3%, n = 14). Most respondents had heard of zoonosis (91.5%, n = 388), recognized animal-to-human disease transmission (96.2%, n = 408), identified viruses (32.1%, n = 136) and believed that dogs pose community risks (82.3%, n = 349) and are sources of parasitic infections (84.9%, n = 360). Conclusion The most participants demonstrated good awareness and positive attitudes toward dog-associated zoonotic diseases. However, gaps in knowledge among some respondents highlight the need for targeted education to promote safer human–animal interactions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of research on awareness levels of high-risk groups within academic settings regarding dog-associated zoonotic diseases. Zoonotic diseases are a major public health challenge in Ethiopia, particularly in rural and semi-urban areas where human-animal interaction is high.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted from April to June 2025, recruiting 424 participants through stratified random sampling with proportional allocation. The majority were undergraduate health students (90.1%, n = 382), followed by security personnel (6.6%, n = 28) and cleaning workers (3.3%, n = 14).
Core methods
Data were collected using a semi-structured questionnaire administered via face-to-face interviews and an online Kobo Toolbox link to assess participants’ knowledge, attitudes, and practices. The data were entered into Excel and analyzed using SPSS version 27, with results presented in tables and narrative summaries; chi-square (χ2) tests were used to assess associations, and p-values less than 0.05 were considered statistically significant.
Main results
Among the 424 respondents, 91.7% (n = 389) were aged 18–29 years, with 48.6% (n = 206) females and 51.4% (n = 216) males; most had college/university education (92.0%, n = 390). Most respondents had heard of zoonosis (91.5%, n = 388), recognized animal-to-human disease transmission (96.2%, n = 408), identified viruses (32.1%, n = 136) and believed that dogs pose community risks (82.3%, n = 349) and are sources of parasitic infections (84.9%, n = 360).
Interpretation and novelty
The most participants demonstrated good awareness and positive attitudes toward dog-associated zoonotic diseases. However, gaps in knowledge among some respondents highlight the need for targeted education to promote safer human–animal interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted educational interventions to improve awareness and promote safer human–animal interactions among high-risk groups in academic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to self-reported data and may be subject to bias. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a cross-sectional survey with no longitudinal follow-up.

埃塞俄比亚西南部塔尔查镇户主对狂犬病的知识态度和行为及相关因素

Knowledge attitudes and practices of household heads toward rabies and associated factors in Tarcha Town, Southwest Ethiopia
作者： Mesfin Feleha Farissa, Chaltu Fikru, Kitesa Biressa, Muluken Mussie Bibbiso
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种致命的人畜共患病毒性疾病,仍然是包括埃塞俄比亚在内的发展中国家的主要公共卫生挑战。在埃塞俄比亚西南部,特别是塔尔查镇,有关 狂犬病 的知识、态度和做法的证据有限。
设计与对象
2023 年 5 月至 6 月,通过简单随机抽样对 548 名户主进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究。与没有狗咬伤史的参与者相比,有过狗咬伤史的参与者采取良好 狂犬病 预防措施的几率更高。
核心方法
使用预先测试的结构化问卷收集数据,输入 EpiData 3.1 版,并使用 SPSS 26 版进行分析。进行双变量和多变量逻辑回归分析,以确定与 KAP 结果相关的因素。
主要结果
Overall, 60.8% of respondents had good knowledge, 52.2% had positive attitudes, and 56.0% demonstrated good practices toward rabies prevention and control. Participants who could reach a veterinary clinic within 30 min had higher odds of having a positive attitude toward rabies prevention and control compared with those who required more than 30 min to reach a veterinary clinic (AOR = 3.17, 95% CI 1.14–8.86).
解释与创新
知识、态度和实践与一些社会人口和获取相关因素显着相关。本研究发现,超过一半的户主对狂犬病有足够的了解、积极的态度和良好的做法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明需要加强研究区域的 狂犬病 意识和健康教育干预措施。这一负担是由有限的社区意识、不良的预防措施以及无法及时获取 暴露后预防 造成的。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。由于该研究基于自我报告的数据和横断面设计,因此证据强度中等。

Knowledge attitudes and practices of household heads toward rabies and associated factors in Tarcha Town, Southwest Ethiopia

Authors: Mesfin Feleha Farissa, Chaltu Fikru, Kitesa Biressa, Muluken Mussie Bibbiso
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is a fatal zoonotic viral disease and remains a major public health challenge in developing countries, including Ethiopia. The burden is driven by limited community awareness, poor preventive practices, and inadequate access to timely post-exposure prophylaxis. Evidence on knowledge, attitudes, and practices toward rabies is limited in southwestern Ethiopia, particularly in Tarcha Town. This study aimed to assess the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) regarding rabies among household heads and to identify factors associated with KAP levels. A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted from May to June 2023 among 548 household heads selected using simple random sampling. Data were collected using a pretested structured questionnaire, entered into EpiData version 3.1, and analyzed using SPSS version 26. Bivariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with KAP outcomes. Overall, 60.8% of respondents had good knowledge, 52.2% had positive attitudes, and 56.0% demonstrated good practices toward rabies prevention and control. Ownership of two or more dogs was significantly associated with good knowledge (AOR = 5.39, 95% CI 2.54–11.48, p < 0.001). Participants who could reach a veterinary clinic within 30 min had higher odds of having a positive attitude toward rabies prevention and control compared with those who required more than 30 min to reach a veterinary clinic (AOR = 3.17, 95% CI 1.14–8.86). Participants with a previous history of dog-bite exposure had higher odds of good rabies-prevention practices than participants without such a history. (AOR = 4.33, 95% CI 2.14–8.74, p < 0.001). This study found that more than half of the household heads had adequate knowledge, positive attitudes, and good practices regarding rabies. However, misconceptions and inadequate preventive practices were still observed among a considerable proportion of participants. Knowledge, attitudes, and practices were significantly associated with several socio-demographic and access-related factors. These findings indicate the need to strengthen rabies awareness and health education interventions in the study area.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies is a fatal zoonotic viral disease and remains a major public health challenge in developing countries, including Ethiopia. Evidence on knowledge, attitudes, and practices toward rabies is limited in southwestern Ethiopia, particularly in Tarcha Town.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted from May to June 2023 among 548 household heads selected using simple random sampling. Participants with a previous history of dog-bite exposure had higher odds of good rabies-prevention practices than participants without such a history.
Core methods
Data were collected using a pretested structured questionnaire, entered into EpiData version 3.1, and analyzed using SPSS version 26. Bivariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with KAP outcomes.
Main results
Overall, 60.8% of respondents had good knowledge, 52.2% had positive attitudes, and 56.0% demonstrated good practices toward rabies prevention and control. Participants who could reach a veterinary clinic within 30 min had higher odds of having a positive attitude toward rabies prevention and control compared with those who required more than 30 min to reach a veterinary clinic (AOR = 3.17, 95% CI 1.14–8.86).
Interpretation and novelty
Knowledge, attitudes, and practices were significantly associated with several socio-demographic and access-related factors. This study found that more than half of the household heads had adequate knowledge, positive attitudes, and good practices regarding rabies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings indicate the need to strengthen rabies awareness and health education interventions in the study area. The burden is driven by limited community awareness, poor preventive practices, and inadequate access to timely post-exposure prophylaxis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. The evidence strength is moderate as the study is based on self-reported data and cross-sectional design.

犬脂肪间充质干细胞制剂的微生物安全评估显示没有可检测到的污染

Microbiological safety assessment of canine adipose derived mesenchymal stem cell preparations reveal no detectable contamination
作者： Marina Prišlin Šimac, Valentina Kunić, Dragan Brnić, Daria Jurković Žilić, Relja Beck, Sanja Duvnjak, Manuela Zadravec, Vjekoslava Kostanić
摘要中文翻译犬脂肪源性间充质干细胞/基质细胞(cAD-MSC)的实验应用已证明治疗对许多以前无法治疗或难以治疗的疾病具有优异的治疗潜力。然而,用于临床应用的cAD-MSC的生产需要体外分离和细胞扩增,从而引入微生物污染的风险。研究表明,储存的间充质干细胞可能被细菌、真菌或病毒污染,导致细胞转化、细胞死亡或持续感染。这最终会损害治疗效果并可能促进治疗诱导的病原体 传播。根据欧洲药品管理局指南,要求将无菌检测作为生产过程的一个组成部分,本研究旨在评估克罗地亚兽医研究所 cAD-MSC 库的无菌性。为了检测临床上最重要的犬病原体,从 32 个 cAD-MSC 制剂中提取核酸,并使用标准分子 PCR 方法进行分析。病原体筛查小组包括狂犬病病毒(狂犬病毒)、犬疱疹病毒(Varicellovirus canidalpha1)、细小病毒(Carnivore protoparvovirus 1)、冠状病毒(Alphacoronavirus 1)、犬瘟热病毒(Morbillivirus canis)、轮状病毒A、钩端螺旋体属、犬布鲁氏菌、伯氏疏螺旋体、无形体spp、埃利希体属、支原体属(嗜血性)和犬新孢子虫。没有检测到上述病原体的测试基因片段,表明cAD-MSC制剂不存在临床上显着的病原体。在细胞分离和扩增过程中严格实施无菌控制对于确保犬类患者的安全细胞治疗至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估犬脂肪源性间充质干细胞 (cAD-MSC) 制剂的微生物安全性,解决此类制剂可能被病原体污染的担忧,从而影响治疗效果并对犬类患者构成风险。
设计与对象
该研究在克罗地亚兽医研究所进行,作为符合欧洲药品管理局指南的无菌评估的一部分,对 32 种 cAD-MSC 制剂进行了测试,以确定是否存在临床上重要的病原体,包括 狂犬病病毒。
核心方法
从 32 个 cAD-MSC 制剂中提取核酸,并使用标准分子 PCR 方法进行分析,以检测制剂中是否存在特定病原体,包括 狂犬病病毒。
主要结果
在 32 个 cAD-MSC 制剂中没有检测到 狂犬病病毒 或任何其他测试病原体的基因片段,表明它们没有受到这些临床上重要的试剂的污染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,cAD-MSC 制剂满足无菌要求,为犬类患者治疗使用的安全性提供了新的确认。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为确保兽医学中基于细胞的疗法的安全性提供了关键信息,特别是 狂犬病病毒,它是犬类群体中一个重大的公共卫生问题。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于测试一组特定的病原体,虽然它提供了 狂犬病病毒 不存在的有力证据,但可能需要进一步测试来确认针对其他潜在污染物的安全性。

Microbiological safety assessment of canine adipose derived mesenchymal stem cell preparations reveal no detectable contamination

Authors: Marina Prišlin Šimac, Valentina Kunić, Dragan Brnić, Daria Jurković Žilić, Relja Beck, Sanja Duvnjak, Manuela Zadravec, Vjekoslava Kostanić
Original abstract (English)
The experimental application of canine adipose-derived mesenchymal stem/stromal cells (cAD-MSC) has demonstrated excellent therapeutic potential in the treatment of a number of previously untreatable or difficult-to-treat diseases. However, the production of cAD-MSC for clinical application necessitates in vitro isolation and cell expansion, introducing the risk of microbiological contamination. Studies indicate that stored MSCs may be contaminated with bacteria, fungi, or viruses, resulting in cell transformation, cell death, or persistent infection. This ultimately compromises therapeutic efficacy and potentially facilitates therapy-induced pathogen transmission. In accordance with the European Medicines Agency guidelines mandating sterility testing as an integral component of the manufacturing process, this study aimed to assess the sterility of the cAD-MSC bank at the Croatian Veterinary Institute. To test for the most clinically significant canine pathogens, nucleic acids were extracted from 32 cAD-MSC preparations and analyzed using standard molecular PCR methods. The pathogen screening panel included rabies virus (Lyssavirus), canine herpesvirus (Varicellovirus canidalpha1), parvovirus (Carnivore protoparvovirus 1), coronavirus (Alphacoronavirus 1), distemper virus (Morbillivirus canis), Rotavirus A, Leptospira spp, Brucella canis, Borrelia burgdorferi sensu lato, Anaplasma spp, Ehrlichia spp, Mycoplasma spp (hemotropic), and Neospora caninum. No tested gene fragments of the aforementioned pathogens were detected, indicating that the cAD-MSC preparations were free from the presence of clinically significant pathogens. Rigorous implementation of sterility control during the cell isolation and expansion process is essential to ensure safe cell-based therapy in canine patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the microbiological safety of canine adipose-derived mesenchymal stem cell (cAD-MSC) preparations, addressing the concern that such preparations may be contaminated with pathogens that could compromise therapeutic efficacy and pose risks to canine patients.
Design and population
The study was conducted at the Croatian Veterinary Institute, where 32 cAD-MSC preparations were tested for the presence of clinically significant pathogens, including rabies virus, as part of a sterility assessment aligned with European Medicines Agency guidelines.
Core methods
Nucleic acids were extracted from the 32 cAD-MSC preparations and analyzed using standard molecular PCR methods to detect the presence of specific pathogens, including rabies virus, in the preparations.
Main results
No gene fragments of rabies virus or any other tested pathogens were detected in the 32 cAD-MSC preparations, indicating that they were free from contamination with these clinically significant agents.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the cAD-MSC preparations met sterility requirements, offering a novel confirmation of their safety for therapeutic use in canine patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical information for ensuring the safety of cell-based therapies in veterinary medicine, particularly for rabies virus, which is a significant public health concern in canine populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to testing a specific set of pathogens, and while it provides strong evidence for the absence of rabies virus, further testing may be required to confirm safety against other potential contaminants.

dặc điểm dịch tễ học các trường hợp điều trị dự phòng Dại sau phơi nhiễm và một số yếu tố liên quan đến thời gian tiếp cận điều trị dự phòng tại thành phố Đồng Nai (địa giới hành chính trước sáp nhập) năm 2025 2025年同奈市(合并前的行政边界)暴露后狂犬病预防病例的流行病学特征以及与获得预防治疗时间相关的因素

Đặc điểm dịch tễ học các trường hợp điều trị dự phòng Dại sau phơi nhiễm và một số yếu tố liên quan đến thời gian tiếp cận điều trị dự phòng tại thành phố Đồng Nai (địa giới hành chính trước sáp nhập) năm 2025 Epidemiological characteristics of post-exposure rabies prophylaxis cases and factors associated with the time to access prophylaxis in Dong Nai City (administrative boundaries prior to merger) in 2025
作者： Hoàn Trần Hữu, Dũng Cao Văn, Trúc Thái Thanh, Toàn Ngô Văn, Toan Ngo Van
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Nghiên cứu này được thực hiện để mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ học của các trường hợp điều trị dự phòng dại sau phơi nhiễm và các yếu tố ảnh hưởng đến thời gian tiếp cận điều trị. Tại Đồng Nai, giai đoạn 2023–2025 ghi nhận 10 ca tử vong do dại, 117 ổ dịch trên động vật và số ca điều trị dự phòng dại sau phơi nhiễm (RPEP) tăng hơn 1,5 lần mỗi năm.
设计与对象
描述性研究回顾了2025年同奈省64个狂犬病疫苗接种点的37.976例暴露后预防病例数据,研究重点包括接触狂犬病暴露后预防的时间因素,包括来自有异常表现动物的暴露病例。
核心方法
采用多变量回归分析以确定导致RPEP治疗延迟的相关因素,定义为暴露后≥2天开始接种。分析因素包括暴露时间、致伤动物类型以及接种服务的获取时间。
主要结果
Trong 37.976 trường hợp RPEP, 53,7% là nam giới và 33,4% thuộc nhóm tuổi 25–49. 31,2% các ca phơi nhiễm xảy ra vào cuối tuần, 19,8% bị mèo cào/cắn, 2,9% bị vết thương vùng đầu–mặt–cổ và 11,4% phơi nhiễm từ động vật có biểu hiện bất thường. Tỷ lệ tiếp cận RPEP muộn là 23,3%.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,周末暴露、猫源性暴露、暴露于有异常行为的动物以及头部、面部、颈部伤口等因素与RPEP治疗延迟相关。这是关于影响同奈市狂犬病暴露后预防治疗及时性的新信息。
科研与公卫意义
本研究结果可为改善狂犬病暴露后预防的宣传策略和疫苗接种服务的分配提供依据,特别是针对暴露于异常动物、头部、面部、颈部伤口以及周末暴露的病例。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于回顾性数据,可能存在记录误差。然而,多变量回归分析的结果表明,确定影响RPEP治疗及时性的相关因素具有较高的可信度。

Đặc điểm dịch tễ học các trường hợp điều trị dự phòng Dại sau phơi nhiễm và một số yếu tố liên quan đến thời gian tiếp cận điều trị dự phòng tại thành phố Đồng Nai (địa giới hành chính trước sáp nhập) năm 2025 Epidemiological characteristics of post-exposure rabies prophylaxis cases and factors associated with the time to access prophylaxis in Dong Nai City (administrative boundaries prior to merger) in 2025

Authors: Hoàn Trần Hữu, Dũng Cao Văn, Trúc Thái Thanh, Toàn Ngô Văn, Toan Ngo Van
Original abstract (English)
Tại Đồng Nai (địa giới hành chính trước sáp nhập), giai đoạn 2023–2025 ghi nhận 10 người tử vong dobệnh Dại, 117 ổ dịch trên động vật và số người điều trị dự phòng Dại sau phơi nhiễm (RPEP) tăng hơn1,5 lần mỗi năm. Mục tiêu của nghiên cứu này nhằm mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ học các trường hợp RPEP vàmột số yếu tố liên quan đến thời gian tiếp cận RPEP tại thành phố Đồng Nai năm 2025. Thiết kế nghiêncứu mô tả, hồi cứu toàn bộ dữ liệu của 37.976 trường hợp RPEP từ 64 điểm tiêm phòng Dại. Phân tíchhồi quy đa biến được sử dụng để xác định yếu tố liên quan đến tiếp cận RPEP muộn (được định nghĩalà bắt đầu tiêm phòng ≥2 ngày sau phơi nhiễm). Trong 37.976 người RPEP; 53,7% là nam giới và 33,4%là nhóm tuổi 25–49. Số người bị phơi nhiễm vào cuối tuần chiếm 31,2%; 19,8% bị mèo cào/cắn; 2,9% bịvết thương đầu–mặt–cổ và 11,4% phơi nhiễm từ động vật có biểu hiện bất thường. Tỷ lệ tiếp cận RPEPmuộn là 23,3%. Các yếu tố liên quan đến tiếp cận RPEP muộn gồm: bị cắn vào cuối tuần (aOR=1,9; 95%CI: 1,77–2,01); phơi nhiễm do mèo (aOR=1,3; 95% CI: 1,25–1,40); phơi nhiễm từ động vật có biểu hiệnbất thường (aOR=1,7; 95% CI: 1,62–1,88), vết cắn vùng đầu–mặt–cổ (aOR=1,5; 95% CI: 1,34–1,73). Đểcải thiện thời gian tiếp cận RPEP, cần truyền thông nguy cơ đối với các trường hợp phơi nhiễm từ độngvật biểu hiện bất thường, vết thương vùng đầu–mặt–cổ, phơi nhiễm do mèo và công khai địa chỉ, thời gianhoạt động cuối tuần của các điểm tiêm Dại.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nghiên cứu này được thực hiện để mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ học của các trường hợp điều trị dự phòng dại sau phơi nhiễm và các yếu tố ảnh hưởng đến thời gian tiếp cận điều trị. Tại Đồng Nai, giai đoạn 2023–2025 ghi nhận 10 ca tử vong do dại, 117 ổ dịch trên động vật và số ca điều trị dự phòng dại sau phơi nhiễm (RPEP) tăng hơn 1,5 lần mỗi năm.
Design and population
Nghiên cứu mô tả, hồi cứu toàn bộ dữ liệu của 37.976 trường hợp RPEP từ 64 điểm tiêm phòng dại tại Đồng Nai trong năm 2025. Nghiên cứu tập trung vào các yếu tố liên quan đến thời gian tiếp cận RPEP, bao gồm cả các ca phơi nhiễm từ động vật có biểu hiện bất thường.
Core methods
Phân tích hồi quy đa biến được sử dụng để xác định các yếu tố liên quan đến việc tiếp cận RPEP muộn, được định nghĩa là bắt đầu tiêm phòng ≥2 ngày sau phơi nhiễm. Các yếu tố được xem xét bao gồm thời gian phơi nhiễm, loại động vật gây thương tích và thời gian tiếp cận dịch vụ tiêm phòng.
Main results
Trong 37.976 trường hợp RPEP, 53,7% là nam giới và 33,4% thuộc nhóm tuổi 25–49. 31,2% các ca phơi nhiễm xảy ra vào cuối tuần, 19,8% bị mèo cào/cắn, 2,9% bị vết thương vùng đầu–mặt–cổ và 11,4% phơi nhiễm từ động vật có biểu hiện bất thường. Tỷ lệ tiếp cận RPEP muộn là 23,3%.
Interpretation and novelty
Kết quả cho thấy các yếu tố như phơi nhiễm vào cuối tuần, do mèo gây ra, từ động vật có biểu hiện bất thường và vết thương vùng đầu–mặt–cổ có liên quan đến việc tiếp cận RPEP muộn. Đây là thông tin mới về các yếu tố ảnh hưởng đến thời gian tiêm phòng dại tại Đồng Nai.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả nghiên cứu có thể hỗ trợ cải thiện chiến lược truyền thông và phân bổ dịch vụ tiêm phòng dại, đặc biệt là đối với các ca phơi nhiễm từ động vật bất thường, vết thương vùng đầu–mặt–cổ và vào cuối tuần.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu dựa trên dữ liệu hồi cứu, có thể tồn tại sai sót trong việc ghi chép. Tuy nhiên, các kết quả được hỗ trợ bởi phân tích hồi quy đa biến, cho thấy mức độ tin cậy cao trong việc xác định các yếu tố liên quan đến thời gian tiếp cận RPEP.
