基于 mRNA 的疗法:从分子发现到临床革命

mRNA-based therapies: From molecular discovery to clinical revolution
作者： de Oliveira A, Sodré F, Louzada- O, Zylberman M, da Silva J, Ferreira L, Menck C
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探索基于 mRNA 的疗法的开发和临床应用,特别是在 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病的背景下,强调对有效和安全的 治疗 方案的需求。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了 mRNA 递送至人体细胞的历史发展,并讨论了其作为基因治疗方法的出现,重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。
核心方法
该研究描述了使用基于脂质的递送系统和可电离的 LNP 将 mRNA 分子引入细胞中,特别提到了体外转录 (IVT) 荧光素酶 mRNA 及其在哺乳动物细胞中的应用。
主要结果
基于 mRNA 的 疫苗,特别是 狂犬病 的临床成功,通过其在保护人类免受病毒病原体侵害方面的高效性和安全性得到了证明,基于 mRNA 的治疗与遗传性转甲状腺素蛋白介导的淀粉样变性相关的多发性神经病的批准标志着 LNP 系统在人类中的首次监管验证。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,基于 mRNA 的疗法可以安全有效地实现全身核酸输送,为治疗遗传性疾病和传染病提供了一种有前途的替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,基于 mRNA 的技术可用于开发针对 狂犬病 和其他疾病的下一代精准疗法,这对改善 治疗 选择和扩大生物医学应用具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究指出,基于 mRNA 的疗法的高成本限制了其广泛的临床应用,尽管有充分的证据证明其有效性和安全性,但仍需要进一步研究来解决技术挑战并提高可及性。

mRNA-based therapies: From molecular discovery to clinical revolution

Authors: de Oliveira A, Sodré F, Louzada- O, Zylberman M, da Silva J, Ferreira L, Menck C
Original abstract (English)
In this review, we revisit the historical development of mRNA delivery into human cells and discuss the emergence of mRNA as a gene-therapy approach. This review focuses on recent advances in the use of mRNA molecules as potential gene therapy tools, highlights current strategies and future perspectives. This study provided one of the earliest proofs of concept that exogenous mRNA could be used to transiently replace defective or absent proteins in vivo.Parallel efforts explored the potential of mRNA as a vaccination platform. These findings highlighted the capacity of mRNA to stimulate antigen-specific cellular immunity and suggested its potential application in infectious disease prevention.Shortly thereafter, the feasibility of mRNA-based cancer vaccines was demonstrated. These results provided early evidence that mRNA vaccination could generate protective antitumor immunity.Together, these pioneering studies established the conceptual and experimental foundations for mRNA-based therapeutics and vaccination, although widespread application remained limited by technical challenges related to mRNA stability, delivery efficiency, and exacerbated inflammation.Concurrently, several post-transcriptional nucleoside modifications in RNA were identified, such as the deamination of adenosines and cytidines, which were shown to influence mRNA translation efficiency and regulation (Kiss, 2001). AbstractFor nearly 50 years, scientists have sought to exploit messenger RNA (mRNA) as a therapeutic tool, overcoming major challenges related to instability, delivery, and inflammatory reactions. A major breakthrough came with the incorporation of modified nucleosides, particularly pseudouridine, during in vitro mRNA synthesis, which markedly improved stability, translation efficiency,...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the development and clinical application of mRNA-based therapies, particularly in the context of rabies virus and rabies disease, highlighting the need for effective and safe treatment options.
Design and population
The study reviewed the historical development of mRNA delivery into human cells and discussed its emergence as a gene-therapy approach, focusing on rabies virus and rabies disease.
Core methods
The study described the use of lipid-based delivery systems and ionizable LNPs to introduce mRNA molecules into cells, with specific mention of in vitro-transcribed (IVT) luciferase mRNA and its application in mammalian cells.
Main results
The clinical success of mRNA-based vaccines, particularly for rabies, was demonstrated through their high efficacy and safety in protecting humans against viral pathogens, and the approval of an mRNA-based therapy for polyneuropathy associated with hereditary transthyretin-mediated amyloidosis marked the first regulatory validation of an LNP system in humans.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrated that mRNA-based therapies can safely and effectively enable systemic nucleic acid delivery, offering a promising alternative for treating genetic disorders and infectious diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that mRNA-based technologies can be used to develop next-generation precision therapies for rabies and other diseases, with implications for improving treatment options and expanding biomedical applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study noted that the high costs of mRNA-based therapies have limited their widespread clinical use, and while the evidence is strong for their efficacy and safety, further research is needed to address technical challenges and improve accessibility.

通过纳入印度尼西亚、坦桑尼亚联合共和国和格鲁吉亚试点的信息,迭代共同开发三方联合风险评估操作流程和工具

Iterative Co‐Development of the Tripartite Joint Risk Assessment Operational Process and Tool Through Incorporating Information From Pilots in Indonesia, the United Republic of Tanzania, and Georgia
作者： Mumford E, Errecaborde K, Wongsathapornchai K, Aguanno R, Belot G, Cole D, Esenamanov V, Pfeiffer D, Prasarnphanich O, Roche X
摘要中文翻译制定标准包括标准、系统、定性和多部门的风险评估流程,促进纳入专家意见和特定部门的风险评估结果。主要发现包括 (1) 政策层面风险框架的价值,(2) 政治意愿的重要性,(3) 地方参与的必要性,(4) 包括所有相关部门的重要性,以及 (5) 明确、逐步的指导的必要性,以促进高效合作和支持国家培训师的培训。对国家人畜共患疾病风险的多部门评估以及不同部门的所有权。他们审查和检查了现有的特定部门风险评估方法和工具的适用性和相关性,并就联合风险评估的以下标准达成一致:提供一个标准、系统的流程,用于对联合优先的人畜共患疾病危害进行基于证据的风险评估。当只有很少和/或质量较差的数据时,要定性并促进纳入专家意见。允许识别数据差距,并在获得更多数据时从迭代使用中受益。促进使用特定部门风险评估的数据和结果。提供逐步的操作指导,以促进有效和公平的合作以及信息、专业知识和观点的共享。前瞻性地联合所有相关部门,即公共卫生(例如卫生部、公共卫生机构)、动物健康(例如兽医服务)、环境(例如野生动物健康、环境保护机构)以及其他部门,例如安全和卫生部门。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种联合风险评估(JRA)的操作工具来管理人畜共患疾病,因为有效的多部门风险评估对于解决人类、动物和生态系统部门的健康风险至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究于 2018 年 3 月至 2019 年 12 月期间在印度尼西亚、坦桑尼亚联合共和国和格鲁吉亚举办了试点研讨会。这些研讨会有 64 名来自中央政府部委以及人类和动物健康、林业、安全和灾害部门的地方工作人员参加。
核心方法
JRA 流程是通过与国家试点和国际专家反复共同开发而制定的。对现有特定部门风险评估方法的适用性进行了审查,并使用标准化反馈表收集参与者的匿名意见。
主要结果
试点研讨会展示了 JRA 流程的实用性,并为其改进提供了重要的反馈。主要调查结果包括政策层面风险框架的价值、政治意愿的重要性、地方参与的必要性以及有效合作的明确、逐步指导的必要性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 JRA 流程在促进多部门合作和改进风险评估工具方面的有效性的证据。新颖之处在于共同开发方法,它将利益相关者的反馈和专家的意见整合到标准化的操作框架中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为多部门风险评估操作工具的开发提供了信息,支持公共卫生、动物健康和环境部门在管理人畜共患疾病方面进行更有效和公平的合作。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于试点研讨会的反馈,而这些反馈可能无法完全代表更广泛的背景。证据是中等的,因为它基于定性反馈和迭代改进而不是定量数据。

Iterative Co‐Development of the Tripartite Joint Risk Assessment Operational Process and Tool Through Incorporating Information From Pilots in Indonesia, the United Republic of Tanzania, and Georgia

Authors: Mumford E, Errecaborde K, Wongsathapornchai K, Aguanno R, Belot G, Cole D, Esenamanov V, Pfeiffer D, Prasarnphanich O, Roche X
Original abstract (English)
Development criteria included a standard, systematic, qualitative, and multisectoral RA process, facilitating incorporation of expert opinion and sector‐specific RA results. Key findings included the (1) value of policy‐level risk framing, (2) importance of political will, (3) need for subnational engagement, (4) importance of including all relevant sectors, and (5) necessity of clear, stepwise guidance to facilitate efficient collaboration and support training of national trainers.ConclusionsCo‐development of the JRA using country pilots and expert consultations allowed for field‐testing and iterative improvement of the tool, ensuring incorporation of user requirements, robust methodology for the joint, multisectoral assessment of national zoonotic disease risks, and ownership by the different sectors. They reviewed and examined existing sector‐specific RA methods and tools for their suitability and relevance and agreed on the following criteria for the joint RA: Provide a standard, systematic process for conducting evidence‐based RA on joint priority zoonotic disease hazards.Be qualitative and facilitate incorporation of expert opinion when only minimal and/or poor‐quality data are available.Allow identification of data gaps, and benefit from iterative use as additional data becomes available.Promote use of data and results from sector‐specific RAs.Provide step‐by‐step operational guidance to facilitate effective and equitable collaboration and sharing of information, expertise, and perspectives.Be prospectively joint across all relevant sectors, that is, public health (e.g., ministries of health, public health agencies), animal health (e.g., veterinary services), environmental (e.g., wildlife health, environmental protection agencies), and others, such as security a...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an operational tool for Joint Risk Assessment (JRA) to manage zoonotic diseases, as effective multisectoral risk assessment is critical for addressing health risks across human, animal, and ecosystem sectors.
Design and population
The study involved pilot workshops in Indonesia, the United Republic of Tanzania, and Georgia, conducted between March 2018 and December 2019. These workshops included 64 participants from central government ministries and subnational staff across human and animal health, forestry, security, and disaster sectors.
Core methods
The JRA process was developed through iterative co-development with country pilots and international experts. Existing sector-specific risk assessment methods were reviewed for suitability, and standardized feedback forms were used to collect anonymous input from participants.
Main results
The pilot workshops demonstrated the utility of the JRA process and provided critical feedback for its improvement. Key findings included the value of policy-level risk framing, the importance of political will, the need for subnational engagement, and the necessity of clear, stepwise guidance for efficient collaboration.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of the JRA process's effectiveness in facilitating multisectoral collaboration and improving risk assessment tools. The novelty lies in the co-development approach that integrates stakeholder feedback and expert input into a standardized, operational framework.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of operational tools for multisectoral risk assessment, supporting more effective and equitable collaboration across public health, animal health, and environmental sectors in managing zoonotic diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on feedback from pilot workshops, which may not fully represent broader contexts. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on qualitative feedback and iterative improvements rather than quantitative data.

联想学习过程中与多巴胺释放相关的纹状体血氧合和全脑动力学

Dopamine release-associated striatal blood oxygenation and whole-brain dynamics during associative learning
作者： Lawen A, Succi I, Lichtman D, Peterka D, Abdus-Saboor I, Kahn I
摘要中文翻译这些发现揭示了多巴胺信号传导与学习过程中大脑活动的广泛变化之间存在密切关系。本研究使用了 18 只雄性 C57/BL6 小鼠(2-3 个月大)。本研究仅使用雄性小鼠。尽管在大鼠中报告了联想学习和多巴胺能信号传导方面的性别差异(69),因此有可能扩展到小鼠,但这项研究仅限于单一性别,以减少受试者间的差异,同时建立和验证这种技术要求很高的实验范式。因此,我们在鼠标侧面放置了一个镜子,倾斜 45°,使前视摄像头也可以捕捉侧视图 (70)。为了检测舔,我们使用了内部算法。伏隔核(NAc)中的多巴胺能信号是基于奖励的学习的核心,但其与全脑血流动力学的关系仍不清楚。通过对清醒、行为正常的小鼠同时进行功能磁共振成像和多巴胺光度测定,我们发现联想学习会引起 NAc 血氧反应的逐渐时间变化,这反映了多巴胺释放动态。这种转变随着提示奖励学习而出现,并延伸到涵盖外侧间隔复合体以及前额叶、岛叶和下丘脑区域的分布式网络。此外,多巴胺瞬变与局部血氧水平依赖性(BOLD)信号紧密对应,奖励值的变化调节 NAc 和其他皮层下结构中延迟的 BOLD 反应。对测量到的多巴胺能信号进行回归可以消除这种与奖励相关的调节,这表明 BOLD 信号跟踪多巴胺能奖励预测。材料和方法动物所有程序均按照道德准则进行...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究联想学习过程中伏隔核中的多巴胺能信号传导与全脑血流动力学之间的关系,因为多巴胺能信号传导是基于奖励的学习的核心,但其与血流动力学的关系仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究是在小鼠身上进行的,所有程序均得到机构动物护理和使用委员会的批准。在适应的最后一天结束时,每只小鼠自愿获得 10 滴水,所有 18 只致力于学习任务的小鼠在 10 天内达到了体重标准。
核心方法
在适应的最后两天,小鼠被放入磁体孔内,并引入扫描仪噪音。使用 Parker 系列 3 微型惰性液体阀控制供水,并使用兼容 MRI 的 12 M-i 相机进行行为监测。使用 ICA-AROMA、白质对预处理数据进行混杂校正;心室;以及全局信号回归、运动回归和空间平滑至 0.5 mm3。
主要结果
全脑时间信噪比(tSNR)显示出皮质和皮质下区域的稳健信号质量,预期的降低主要限于下边界和腹外侧边界。与人类研究相比,小鼠 HRF 的特点是响应较短,导致数据采样相对不足。学习是在个体动物层面而不是仅仅在群体层面进行评估的。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了跨大脑区域信号质量的稳健性以及由于小鼠 HRF 较短而导致的数据采样不足。这项研究为联想学习期间全脑血流动力学的动态提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以帮助理解学习过程中全脑血流动力学反应,这可能对动物研究中更准确的神经活动模型的开发产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一性别以减少变异性,并且由于技术限制,数据采样不足。证据是中等的,因为该研究提供了详细的观察结果,但缺乏更广泛的普遍性。

Dopamine release-associated striatal blood oxygenation and whole-brain dynamics during associative learning

Authors: Lawen A, Succi I, Lichtman D, Peterka D, Abdus-Saboor I, Kahn I
Original abstract (English)
These findings reveal a close relationship between dopamine signaling and widespread changes in brain activity during learning. Eighteen male C57/BL6 mice (2–3 months old) were used in this study. Male mice were used exclusively in this study. Although sex differences in associative learning and dopaminergic signaling have been reported in rats (69) and thus could potentially extend to mice, this study was restricted to a single sex in order to reduce inter-subject variability while establishing and validating this technically demanding experimental paradigm. Therefore, we incorporated a mirror positioned by the mouse’s side and tilted at 45°, allowing the front-view camera to also capture a side view (70).To detect licks, we used an in-house algorithm. Dopaminergic signaling in the nucleus accumbens (NAc) is central to reward-based learning, but its relationship to brain-wide hemodynamics remains unclear. Using concurrent fMRI and dopamine photometry in awake, behaving mice, we reveal that associative learning induces a gradual temporal shift in NAc blood oxygenation responses that mirrors dopamine release dynamics. This shift emerges with cue-reward learning and extends across a distributed network encompassing the lateral septal complex, as well as prefrontal, insular, and hypothalamic regions. Further, dopamine transients tightly correspond with local blood oxygenation level dependent (BOLD) signals, and variations in reward value modulate delayed BOLD responses in both the NAc and additional subcortical structures. Regressing out the measured dopaminergic signal abolishes this reward-related modulation, suggesting that BOLD signals track dopaminergic reward prediction. MATERIALS AND METHODSAnimalsAll procedures were conducted in accordance with the ethical guideli...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the relationship between dopaminergic signaling in the nucleus accumbens and brain-wide hemodynamics during associative learning, as dopaminergic signaling is central to reward-based learning but its relationship to hemodynamics remains unclear.
Design and population
The study was conducted on mice, with all procedures approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee. By the end of the last day of habituation, each mouse voluntarily acquired 10 drops of water, and all 18 mice dedicated for the learning task reached weight criteria within 10 days.
Core methods
During the last two days of habituation, mice were placed inside the magnet bore with scanner noises introduced. Water delivery was controlled using a Parker Series 3 Miniature Inert Liquid Valve, and behavioral monitoring was done with an MRI-compatible 12 M-i camera. Pre-processed data was confound-corrected using ICA-AROMA, white matter; ventricle; and global signal regression, motion regression, and spatial smoothing to 0.5 mm3.
Main results
Whole-brain temporal signal-to-noise ratio (tSNR) demonstrated robust signal quality across cortical and subcortical regions, with expected reductions primarily confined to inferior and ventrolateral boundaries. The mouse HRF, characterized by shorter responses, resulted in relatively under-sampled data compared to human studies. Learning was assessed at the individual-animal level rather than solely at the group level.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the robustness of signal quality across brain regions and the under-sampling of data due to the shorter HRF in mice. This study provides novel insights into the dynamics of brain-wide hemodynamics during associative learning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the understanding of brain-wide hemodynamic responses during learning processes, which may have implications for the development of more accurate models of neural activity in animal studies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was restricted to a single sex to reduce variability, and the data was under-sampled due to technical constraints. The evidence is moderate, as the study provides detailed observations but lacks broader generalizability.

核糖体重塑驱动病毒感染和细胞应激期间的翻译适应

Ribosome remodeling drives translation adaptation during viral infection and cellular stress
作者： Tsai H, Liu L, Fleming R, Mintseris J, Ekanayake D, Guo J, Gu X, Gygi S, Lee A
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究核糖体重塑如何有助于病毒感染和细胞应激期间的翻译适应,基于核糖体是蛋白质合成的核心并且可以根据细胞变化进行重新编程的理解。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注非节段负义(NNS)病毒,特别检查 狂犬病病毒(RABV)感染对人类细胞和初级皮质神经元的影响。它还包括麻疹病毒和 VSV-G 假型慢病毒的实验。
核心方法
研究人员使用化学交联和质谱(XL-MS)来识别重构核糖体上的蛋白质相互作用。他们还进行了分子结构预测,并应用高严格尿素 治疗 和 RNase 消化来分离直接与核糖体相互作用的蛋白质。
主要结果
感染后,80S 核糖体和多核糖体上的 rpL40 水平增加。观察到 rpL40 和 rpS3 之间的相互作用,这是 VSV 感染细胞核糖体特有的。在麻疹病毒和慢病毒感染中也注意到了 rpL40 的存在。
解释与创新
作者解释说,核糖体重塑是 NNS 病毒在 宿主 压力下用于增强病毒蛋白质合成的保守机制。这一发现凸显了病毒已经采用的先前未被识别的内源核糖体重塑程序。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了关于病毒如何操纵 宿主 核糖体来优先考虑其自身蛋白质合成的见解,这可以为 抗病毒药物 治疗策略提供信息,并提高对感染期间 宿主 与病毒相互作用的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对特定 NNS 病毒的关注和体外方法的使用而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于质谱分析和免疫印迹,其中一些结果基于间接测量。

Ribosome remodeling drives translation adaptation during viral infection and cellular stress

Authors: Tsai H, Liu L, Fleming R, Mintseris J, Ekanayake D, Guo J, Gu X, Gygi S, Lee A
Original abstract (English)
Altogether, these results illustrate an underexplored function of this essential machinery, beyond its core role in peptide bond synthesis, in selective mRNA translation. Furthermore, these findings highlight endogenous shifts in ribosome composition as a critical pathway for gene regulation, with widespread implications on cell specification, adaptation, and pathogen infection. Results Ribosome remodeling is a conserved response to NNS virus infection Viruses manipulate non-canonical host mechanisms to reprogram gene expression machinery, and defining these pathways has revealed previously unrecognized yet critical modes of cellular gene regulation13–15. We observed the greatest change in ribosome occupancy upon infection by the large ribosomal subunit protein rpL40. In agreement with the mass spectrometry results, rpL40 levels increased 2-fold on 80S ribosomes and polysomes upon VSV infection as measured by immunoblotting (Fig. Summary The ribosome is the highly conserved molecular machine that decodes mRNAs during protein synthesis. While traditionally thought to consist of a uniform set of proteins, here we discover that ribosome composition is reprogrammed to adapt to intrinsic and external cellular perturbations. During infection of human cells by non-segmented negative-sense viruses, viral entry into cells recruits the large ribosomal subunit protein rpL40 to a noncanonical site on the small subunit of 80S ribosomes near the mRNA entry site. These specialized ribosomes preferentially bind viral mRNAs to drive enhanced viral protein synthesis that is critical for replication under host pressures. Unexpectedly, we find that viruses have co-opted this translation pathway from a previously unrecognized endogenous ribosome remodeling program in which metabolic stress...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how ribosome remodeling contributes to translation adaptation during viral infection and cellular stress, building on the understanding that ribosomes are central to protein synthesis and can be reprogrammed in response to cellular changes.
Design and population
The study focused on non-segmented negative-sense (NNS) viruses, specifically examining the effects of rabies virus (RABV) infection on human cells and primary cortical neurons. It also included experiments with measles virus and a VSV-G pseudotyped lentivirus.
Core methods
The researchers used chemical crosslinking and mass spectrometry (XL-MS) to identify protein interactions on remodeled ribosomes. They also performed molecular structure prediction and applied high-stringency urea treatment and RNase digestion to isolate direct ribosome-interacting proteins.
Main results
Upon infection, rpL40 levels increased on 80S ribosomes and polysomes. The interaction between rpL40 and rpS3 was observed, and this was specific to ribosomes from VSV-infected cells. rpL40 occupancy was also noted in measles virus and lentivirus infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that ribosome remodeling is a conserved mechanism used by NNS viruses to enhance viral protein synthesis under host pressures. This discovery highlights a previously unrecognized endogenous ribosome remodeling program that viruses have co-opted.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into how viruses manipulate host ribosomes to prioritize their own protein synthesis, which could inform strategies for antiviral therapies and improve understanding of host-virus interactions during infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on specific NNS viruses and the use of in vitro methods. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on mass spectrometry and immunoblotting, with some results based on indirect measurements.

超越种群规模:首尔实施 TNR 期间自由漫游猫种群结构的长期变化

Beyond population size: long-term changes in free-roaming cat population structure during TNR implementation in Seoul
作者： Cho Y, Byun J, Hwang B, Lee Y
摘要中文翻译摘要背景城市环境中的自由漫游猫(Felis catus)通常通过诱捕-绝育-回归(TNR)计划进行管理,但这些措施的长期种群影响评估仍然不一致,特别是在考虑种群规模和结构动态时。目的本研究旨在评估在韩国首尔公共 TNR 计划背景下观察到的自由漫游猫种群和种群结构模式的长期变化,使用多种人口和结构指标,特别是绝育率、方法从 2017 年到 2025 年,在 17 个固定调查地点进行了一项纵向观察研究。在探索性地点水平比较中,保持高绝育率的地点显示出与较低的小猫比例和较高的表观保留率一致的模式,而绝育率下降的地点则显示出与较大的表观更替和较高的小猫比例一致的模式。结论这些发现描述了在首尔公共 TNR 计划实施期间观察到的种群结构模式,包括小猫数量减少并随着时间的推移增加绝育概率。每两年进行一次重复实地调查,每个地点至少访问五次。通过非侵入性摄影个体识别计算种群规模、绝育率、小猫比例和表观保留率。负二项式和二项式混合效应模型评估了时间趋势和土地利用差异。逻辑混合效应模型通过基线绝育状态评估表观保留,并使用一年内的遭遇历史来估计检测概率。结果总体种群规模和密度显示出下降...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解首尔实施 TNR 计划期间自由流浪猫种群结构的长期变化,重点关注绝育率和小猫比例如何影响种群动态。
设计与对象
从 2017 年起,首尔进行了一项纵向研究,涉及根据土地利用分类选择的 17 个地点。优先选择密度接近全市平均水平的地点,以避免过度代表极端条件。
核心方法
使用非侵入性摄影个体识别来计算种群规模、绝育率、小猫比例和表观保留率。逻辑混合效应模型通过基线绝育状态评估表观保留,并使用一年内的遭遇历史来估计检测概率。
主要结果
Overall population size and density showed a decreasing temporal pattern, while neutering rates generally increased and kitten proportions generally decreased. Cats neutered at baseline had significantly higher odds of apparent retention than non-neutered cats (OR = 3.06, 95% CI: 2.07–4.53).
解释与创新
研究结果描述了首尔公共 TNR 计划实施期间观察到的种群结构模式,包括随着时间的推移,小猫数量减少和绝育概率增加。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解 TNR 计划如何影响野猫种群动态,并深入了解绝育在减少小猫比例和增加表观保留方面的有效性。
局限与证据强度
由于几个单元中的样本量较小,站点年份估计被视为补充,并且耳尖状态未被用作纵向重新识别的主要匹配标准。

Beyond population size: long-term changes in free-roaming cat population structure during TNR implementation in Seoul

Authors: Cho Y, Byun J, Hwang B, Lee Y
Original abstract (English)
AbstractBackgroundFree-roaming cats (Felis catus) in urban environments are commonly managed through trap–neuter–return (TNR) programs, yet the long-term population effects of these measures remain inconsistently evaluated, particularly when considering both population size and structural dynamics.ObjectivesThis study aimed to assess long-term changes in free-roaming cat populations and population-structure patterns observed in the context of public TNR program in Seoul, Republic of Korea, using multiple demographic and structural indicators, specifically neutering rate, kitten proportion, and apparent retention.MethodsA longitudinal observational study was conducted across 17 fixed survey sites from 2017 to 2025. In exploratory site-level comparisons, sites maintaining high neutering rates showed patterns consistent with lower kitten proportions and higher apparent retention, whereas sites with declining neutering rates showed patterns consistent with greater apparent turnover and higher kitten proportions.ConclusionsThese findings describe population-structure patterns observed during implementation of Seoul's public TNR program, including reduced kitten counts and increased neutering probability over time. Repeated field surveys were performed biennially, with at least five visits per site. Population size, neutering rate, kitten proportion, and apparent retention were calculated via non-invasive photographic individual identification. Negative binomial and binomial mixed-effects models evaluated temporal trends and land-use differences. A logistic mixed effects model assessed apparent retention by baseline neutering status, and within-year encounter histories were used to estimate detection probability.ResultsOverall population size and density showed a decreasing...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand long-term changes in free-roaming cat population structure during the implementation of a TNR program in Seoul, focusing on how neutering rates and kitten proportions affect population dynamics.
Design and population
A longitudinal study was conducted in Seoul from 2017 onward, involving 17 sites selected based on land-use classifications. Sites with densities approximating the citywide average were preferentially chosen to avoid overrepresentation of extreme conditions.
Core methods
Population size, neutering rate, kitten proportion, and apparent retention were calculated using non-invasive photographic individual identification. A logistic mixed effects model assessed apparent retention by baseline neutering status, and within-year encounter histories were used to estimate detection probability.
Main results
Overall population size and density showed a decreasing temporal pattern, while neutering rates generally increased and kitten proportions generally decreased. Cats neutered at baseline had significantly higher odds of apparent retention than non-neutered cats (OR = 3.06, 95% CI: 2.07–4.53).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings describe population-structure patterns observed during the implementation of Seoul's public TNR program, including reduced kitten counts and increased neutering probability over time.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform the understanding of how TNR programs can influence feral cat population dynamics, offering insights into the effectiveness of neutering in reducing kitten proportions and increasing apparent retention.
Limitations and evidence strength
Site-year estimates were treated as supplementary due to small sample sizes in several cells, and ear-tip status was not used as the primary matching criterion for longitudinal re-identification.

狂犬病病毒 和相关狂犬病病毒的生物安全:诊断和研究实验室的风险、预防措施和知识差距

Biosafety of rabies virus and related lyssaviruses: Risks, precautions, and knowledge gaps in diagnostic and research laboratories
作者： Stuart Dean Blacksell, Khanh Kim Le, Paola De Benedictis, Morgane Gourlaouen
摘要中文翻译狂犬病毒在哺乳动物中引起 狂犬病,这是一种病毒性脑脊髓炎,一旦出现临床症状,几乎总是致命的,并且可以传播给人类。鉴于如此高的死亡率,在实验室处理潜在感染性材料期间必须采取严格的生物安全措施。本次审查通过检查主要风险因素、传播 途径以及现有预防措施的有效性,批判性地评估了 狂犬病 诊断和研究实验室当前的生物安全实践。我们对与处理 狂犬病病毒 和相关狂犬病病毒相关的生物安全风险的文献进行了全面审查,包括个人防护设备的使用、样品处理程序以及病毒灭活的化学和物理方法。特别关注实验室获得性 狂犬病 的记录病例及其对实验室安全的影响。尽管实验室获得性感染仍然很少见,但它们的发生凸显了当前生物安全实践的重要差距。消毒和灭活方法的有效性差异很大,特别是在资源有限的环境中,并且关键诊断程序(例如直接荧光抗体测试)需要在生物安全限制下进行验证。在感染剂量、净化方法的有效性和诊断标准化方面仍然存在重大知识差距。加强循证生物安全方案和验证灭活方法对于确保更安全的 狂犬病 诊断和支持有效的 狂犬病 控制至关重要,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本次审查通过检查主要风险因素、传播 途径以及现有预防措施的有效性,批判性地评估了 狂犬病 诊断和研究实验室当前的生物安全实践。
证据基础与方法
我们对与处理 狂犬病病毒 和相关狂犬病病毒相关的生物安全风险的文献进行了全面审查,包括个人防护设备的使用、样品处理程序以及病毒灭活的化学和物理方法。
共识与结论
尽管实验室获得性感染仍然很少见,但它们的发生凸显了当前生物安全实践的重要差距。消毒和灭活方法的有效性差异很大,特别是在资源有限的环境中,并且关键诊断程序(例如直接荧光抗体测试)需要在生物安全限制下进行验证。
争议与缺口
在感染剂量、净化方法的有效性和诊断标准化方面仍然存在重大知识差距。
科研与实践启示
加强循证生物安全方案和验证灭活方法对于确保更安全的 狂犬病 诊断和支持有效的 狂犬病 控制至关重要,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。

Biosafety of rabies virus and related lyssaviruses: Risks, precautions, and knowledge gaps in diagnostic and research laboratories

Authors: Stuart Dean Blacksell, Khanh Kim Le, Paola De Benedictis, Morgane Gourlaouen
Original abstract (English)
Lyssaviruses cause rabies in mammals, a viral encephalomyelitis that is almost always fatal once clinical symptoms develop and can be transmitted to humans. Given this high fatality rate, strict biosafety measures are essential during laboratory handling of potentially infectious materials. This review critically assesses current biosafety practices in rabies diagnostic and research laboratories by examining principal risk factors, transmission pathways, and the effectiveness of existing preventive measures. We conducted a comprehensive review of the literature on biosafety risks associated with handling rabies virus and related lyssaviruses, including the use of personal protective equipment, sample handling procedures, and chemical and physical methods for virus inactivation. Particular attention was given to documented cases of laboratory-acquired rabies and their implications for laboratory safety. Although laboratory-acquired infections remain rare, their occurrence highlights important gaps in current biosafety practices. The effectiveness of disinfection and inactivation methods varies widely, especially in resource-limited settings, and key diagnostic procedures, such as the direct fluorescent antibody test, require validation under biosafety constraints. Significant knowledge gaps remain regarding infectious dose, the effectiveness of decontamination methods, and diagnostic standardisation. Strengthening evidence-based biosafety protocols and validating inactivation methods are essential to ensure safer rabies diagnostics and to support effective rabies control, particularly in low-resource settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review critically assesses current biosafety practices in rabies diagnostic and research laboratories by examining principal risk factors, transmission pathways, and the effectiveness of existing preventive measures.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a comprehensive review of the literature on biosafety risks associated with handling rabies virus and related lyssaviruses, including the use of personal protective equipment, sample handling procedures, and chemical and physical methods for virus inactivation.
Consensus and conclusions
Although laboratory-acquired infections remain rare, their occurrence highlights important gaps in current biosafety practices. The effectiveness of disinfection and inactivation methods varies widely, especially in resource-limited settings, and key diagnostic procedures, such as the direct fluorescent antibody test, require validation under biosafety constraints.
Controversies and gaps
Significant knowledge gaps remain regarding infectious dose, the effectiveness of decontamination methods, and diagnostic standardisation.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening evidence-based biosafety protocols and validating inactivation methods are essential to ensure safer rabies diagnostics and to support effective rabies control, particularly in low-resource settings.

RVG29 功能化纳米载体用于神经退行性疾病的脑靶向治疗:机制、治疗进展和转化视角。

RVG29-functionalized nanocarriers for brain-targeted therapy of neurodegenerative diseases: mechanisms, therapeutic advances, and translational perspectives.
作者： Sonam M. Gandhi, Saraswati Patel, Nitin G. Haswani, Shadma Wahab, Zia-Ul- Sabah, Devesh U. Kapoor, Zia-ul-Sabah
摘要中文翻译神经退行性疾病(NDs)影响全球超过55亿人,由于人口老龄化,预计到2050年这一数字将超过139亿。阿尔茨海默病(AD)占痴呆病例的60-70%,而帕金森病(PD)影响全球超过10亿人,凸显了这些进行性神经系统疾病日益增长的临床和社会经济负担。有效的治疗治疗AD、PD、肌萎缩侧索硬化症(ALS)及相关中枢神经系统疾病仍受到血脑屏障(BBB)、脑内积累不足、系统快速清除以及脱靶毒性等因素的阻碍。RVG29是一种由狂犬病病毒糖蛋白衍生的29个氨基酸肽,因其对神经元和脑内皮细胞上烟碱型乙酰胆碱受体的高亲和力,已成为一种有前景的脑靶向配体。本文批判性地探讨了RVG29介导的受体依赖性BBB跨细胞转运、细胞内运输和神经元摄取的机制。此外,还全面评估了RVG29功能化的脂质纳米颗粒、聚合物纳米颗粒、外泌体、生物模拟纳米载体、胶束、无机纳米结构以及混合系统在物理化学特性、靶向效率、治疗效果和安全性方面的表现。总体而言,这些纳米载体显示出增强的BBB穿透能力,提高了药物在病变脑区的积累,并有效传递小分子、基因、mRNA和siRNA。通过调节氧化应激、神经炎症、蛋白质聚集、铁死亡和线粒体功能障碍,RVG29工程化的纳米载体提供了显著的神经保护作用,并具有良好的生物相容性和最小的免疫应答。尽管取得了这些进展,仍需解决大规模生产、肽稳定性、监管批准和长期生物安全性等挑战,以促进其作为精准神经治疗药物的临床转化。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 RVG29 功能化纳米载体用于神经退行性疾病的脑靶向治疗,包括阿尔茨海默病 (AD)、帕金森病 (PD) 和肌萎缩侧索硬化症 (ALS)。核心问题是这些纳米载体如何克服将治疗剂穿过血脑屏障(BBB)并进入大脑的挑战。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 RVG29 功能化纳米载体进行了全面分析,检查了它们的 BBB 转胞吞作用、细胞内运输和神经元摄取的机制。它评估各种纳米载体类型,包括脂质纳米颗粒、聚合物纳米颗粒、外泌体、仿生纳米载体、胶束、无机纳米结构和混合系统的理化特性、靶向效率、治疗功效和安全性。
共识与结论
RVG29 工程纳米载体表现出增强的 BBB 渗透性、增加患病大脑区域的药物积累以及小分子、基因、mRNA 和 siRNA 的有效递送。这些纳米载体通过调节氧化应激、神经炎症、蛋白质聚集、铁死亡和线粒体功能障碍提供显着的神经保护作用,具有良好的生物相容性和最小的免疫原性。
争议与缺口
尽管取得了令人鼓舞的进展,但大规模生产、肽稳定性、监管审批和长期生物安全等挑战仍未解决。该综述强调需要进一步研究来解决这些问题并促进 RVG29 功能化纳米载体作为精准神经治疗药物的临床转化。
科研与实践启示
为了推进RVG29功能化纳米载体的临床应用,未来的研究应重点关注优化其制造工艺、增强肽稳定性以及进行长期安全性研究。此外,需要制定监管框架以支持其批准作为神经退行性疾病的治疗药物。

RVG29-functionalized nanocarriers for brain-targeted therapy of neurodegenerative diseases: mechanisms, therapeutic advances, and translational perspectives.

Authors: Sonam M. Gandhi, Saraswati Patel, Nitin G. Haswani, Shadma Wahab, Zia-Ul- Sabah, Devesh U. Kapoor, Zia-ul-Sabah
Original abstract (English)
Neurodegenerative diseases (NDs) affect more than 55 million people worldwide, with this number projected to exceed 139 million by 2050 due to population aging. Alzheimer's disease (AD) accounts for 60-70% of dementia cases, while Parkinson's disease (PD) affects over 10 million individuals globally, highlighting the growing clinical and socioeconomic burden of these progressive neurological disorders. Effective treatment of AD, PD, amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS), and related central nervous system disorders remains hindered by the blood-brain barrier (BBB), poor brain accumulation, rapid systemic clearance, and off-target toxicity. RVG29, a 29-amino-acid peptide derived from rabies virus glycoprotein, has emerged as a promising brain-targeting ligand because of its high affinity for nicotinic acetylcholine receptors expressed on neuronal and brain endothelial cells. This review critically examines the mechanisms underlying RVG29-mediated receptor-dependent BBB transcytosis, intracellular trafficking, and neuronal uptake. Furthermore, it comprehensively evaluates RVG29-functionalized lipid nanoparticles, polymeric nanoparticles, exosomes, biomimetic nanocarriers, micelles, inorganic nanostructures, and hybrid systems with respect to their physicochemical characteristics, targeting efficiency, therapeutic efficacy, and safety. Collectively, these nanocarriers demonstrate enhanced BBB penetration, increased drug accumulation in diseased brain regions, and efficient delivery of small molecules, genes, mRNA, and siRNA. By modulating oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, protein aggregation, ferroptosis, and mitochondrial dysfunction, RVG29-engineered nanocarriers provide significant neuroprotection with favorable biocompatibility and minimal immunogenicity. Despite these advances, challenges related to large-scale manufacturing, peptide stability, regulatory approval, and long-term biosafety must be addressed to facilitate their clinical translation as precision neurotherapeutics.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the use of RVG29-functionalized nanocarriers for brain-targeted therapy of neurodegenerative diseases, including Alzheimer's disease (AD), Parkinson's disease (PD), and amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). The central question is how these nanocarriers can overcome the challenges of delivering therapeutic agents across the blood-brain barrier (BBB) and into the brain.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of RVG29-functionalized nanocarriers, examining their mechanisms of BBB transcytosis, intracellular trafficking, and neuronal uptake. It evaluates various nanocarrier types, including lipid nanoparticles, polymeric nanoparticles, exosomes, biomimetic nanocarriers, micelles, inorganic nanostructures, and hybrid systems, with respect to their physicochemical characteristics, targeting efficiency, therapeutic efficacy, and safety.
Consensus and conclusions
RVG29-engineered nanocarriers demonstrate enhanced BBB penetration, increased drug accumulation in diseased brain regions, and efficient delivery of small molecules, genes, mRNA, and siRNA. These nanocarriers provide significant neuroprotection by modulating oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, protein aggregation, ferroptosis, and mitochondrial dysfunction, with favorable biocompatibility and minimal immunogenicity.
Controversies and gaps
Despite the promising advances, challenges such as large-scale manufacturing, peptide stability, regulatory approval, and long-term biosafety remain unresolved. The review highlights the need for further research to address these issues and facilitate the clinical translation of RVG29-functionalized nanocarriers as precision neurotherapeutics.
Research and practice implications
To advance the clinical application of RVG29-functionalized nanocarriers, future research should focus on optimizing their manufacturing processes, enhancing peptide stability, and conducting long-term safety studies. Additionally, regulatory frameworks need to be developed to support their approval as therapeutic agents for neurodegenerative diseases.

mRNA 疫苗预防传染病:基于临床试验结果评估有效性和安全性的国际经验回顾

mRNA vaccines for the prevention of infectious diseases: A review of international experience in evaluating efficacy and safety based on clinical trial results
作者： A. Yu. Butirskiy, A. S. Korovkin, Д. В. Горенков, Вадим Анатольевич Меркулов, D. Gorenkov, V. Merkulov
摘要中文翻译用于创建抗传染病疫苗的mRNA平台技术在COVID-19大流行期间被引入临床实践;然而,mRNA 疫苗 的临床使用积累的经验需要与其他传染病相关的系统化。因此,总结此类 mRNA 疫苗 在对照临床试验中的有效性和安全性数据,对于证实此类免疫生物医药产品的监管评估方法相关。回顾 mRNA 疫苗 用于预防呼吸道合胞病毒感染(RSV 感染)、巨细胞病毒感染(CMV 感染)、季节性流感、寨卡热和 狂犬病 的临床试验结果,总结其有效性和安全性数据,确定与使用替代终点和样本量相关的局限性。作者分析了 PubMed、eLIBRARY.RU 中的出版物和 ClinicalTrials.gov 中的记录,描述了 2016 年至 2026 年 mRNA 疫苗 用于预防传染病(不包括 COVID-19)的对照临床试验的结果。仅证实了 mRNA-1345 疫苗 针对 RSV 感染的临床疗效:83.7%(针对有 2 种症状的发作)和82.4%(有 3 种症状的发作);安全状况被认为是令人满意的。对于针对季节性流感的 mRNA 疫苗,在血凝抑制测定中证实了良好的安全性和显着的血清转化;体液免疫参数与授权的疫苗相当。针对 CMV 感染的 mRNA-1647 疫苗 在三剂量方案后,诱导持久的 疫苗 激活 T 细胞免疫,并具有良好的安全性。在一项 疫苗 用于预防寨卡热(mRNA-1325、mRNA-1893)的研究中,在具有不同基线黄病毒血清状态的参与者中证明了 疫苗 后存在中和抗体(在中和测定中);安全状况被认为是有利的。针对 狂犬病 的 mRNA 疫苗 CV7201 在皮内给药后 71% 的研究参与者和肌肉内给药后 46% 的研究参与者中诱导了病毒中和抗体的保护性滴度;局部和全身不良事件 (AE) 的 发病率 较高(分别为 97% 和 78%)。大多数研究都显示出与使用替代终点评估疗效相关的局限性,并且样本量不足以检测罕见的不良事件。mRNA 疫苗 用于预防 RSV 和 CMV 感染、季节性流感、寨卡热和 狂犬病 的对照临床试验结果证明了其潜在功效和可接受的安全性;然而,大多数产品的评估都是基于替代终点和小样本量,这是所进行的研究的主要局限性。研究结果证实需要使用终点(临床疗效、替代标志物)和上市后安全监测(主要针对局部和全身不良事件)进行进一步研究。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。核心问题是评估 mRNA 疫苗 相对于 疫苗 的有效性和安全性,特别强调临床试验及其结果。
证据基础与方法
作者分析了 PubMed、eLIBRARY.RU 中的出版物和 ClinicalTrials.gov 中的记录,描述了 2016 年至 2026 年 mRNA 疫苗 用于预防传染病(不包括 COVID-19)的对照临床试验的结果。该综述包括关于 RSV 感染、CMV 感染、季节性流感、寨卡热和 狂犬病 的研究。该方法涉及临床试验数据的系统分析,并注意替代终点和样本量限制。
共识与结论
针对 狂犬病 的 mRNA 疫苗 CV7201 在皮内给药后 71% 的研究参与者和肌肉内给药后 46% 的研究参与者中诱导了病毒中和抗体的保护性滴度。安全性被认为是有利的,但局部和全身不良事件 (AE) 的 发病率 很高(分别为 97% 和 78%)。仅证实了mRNA-1345 疫苗针对RSV感染的临床功效,但这与狂犬病没有直接关系。尽管该综述指出使用替代终点和小样本量评估疗效的局限性,但 mRNA 疫苗 相对于 狂犬病 的安全性被认为是令人满意的。
争议与缺口
大多数 mRNA 疫苗(包括 狂犬病)用于预防传染病的评估都是基于替代终点和小样本量,这是所进行的研究的主要局限性。该审查强调,替代终点的使用可能无法完全捕捉临床疗效,并且没有足够的数据来检测罕见的不良事件。此外,该审查指出,mRNA 疫苗 相对于 狂犬病 的安全性虽然被认为是有利的,但与局部和全身不良事件的高 发病率 相关。
科研与实践启示
研究结果证实需要使用临床疗效终点和替代标志物进行进一步研究。强调上市后安全监测,特别是局部和全身不良事件。该综述还表明,在未来的研究中解决替代终点和小样本量的局限性的重要性,以改进 mRNA 疫苗 对 狂犬病 预防的评估。

mRNA vaccines for the prevention of infectious diseases: A review of international experience in evaluating efficacy and safety based on clinical trial results

Authors: A. Yu. Butirskiy, A. S. Korovkin, Д. В. Горенков, Вадим Анатольевич Меркулов, D. Gorenkov, V. Merkulov
Original abstract (English)
The mRNA platform technology for creating vaccines against infectious diseases was introduced into clinical practice during the COVID-19 pandemic; however, the accumulated experience of clinical use of mRNA vaccines requires systematization in relation to other infectious diseases. Therefore, summarizing data on the efficacy and safety of such mRNA vaccines in controlled clinical trials is relevant for substantiating approaches to the regulatory evaluation of immunobiological medicinal products of this class. To review the results of clinical trials of mRNA vaccines for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus infection (RSV infection), cytomegalovirus infection (CMV infection), seasonal influenza, Zika fever, and rabies to summarize data on their efficacy and safety, identifying the limitations associated with the use of surrogate endpoints and sample size. The authors analyzed publications in PubMed, eLIBRARY.RU and records in ClinicalTrials.gov describing the results of controlled clinical trials of mRNA vaccines for the prevention of infectious diseases (excluding COVID-19) from 2016 to 2026. Clinical efficacy was confirmed only for the mRNA-1345 vaccine against RSV infection: 83.7% (for episodes with 2 symptoms) and 82.4% (for episodes with 3 symptoms); the safety profile was considered satisfactory. For mRNA vaccines against seasonal influenza, a favorable safety profile and pronounced seroconversion in the hemagglutination inhibition assay were confirmed; the humoral immunity parameters were comparable to those of licensed vaccines. The mRNA-1647 vaccine against CMV infection, after a three-dose regimen, induced a durable immune response with the activation of T-cell immunity, along with a favorable safety profile. In a study of vaccines for the prevention of Zika fever (mRNA-1325, mRNA-1893), the presence of neutralizing antibodies (in a neutralization assay) after vaccination was demonstrated in participants with different baseline flavivirus serostatus; the safety profile was considered favorable. The mRNA vaccine CV7201 against rabies induced protective titers of virus-neutralizing antibodies in 71% of study participants after intradermal administration and in 46% after intramuscular administration; the incidence of local and systemic adverse events (AEs) was high (97% and 78%, respectively). Most studies were shown to have limitations associated with the assessment of efficacy using surrogate endpoints and an insufficient sample size to detect rare adverse events. The results of controlled clinical trials of mRNA vaccines for the prevention of RSV and CMV infection, seasonal influenza, Zika fever, and rabies demonstrated their potential efficacy and acceptable safety profile; however, the evaluation of most products is based on surrogate endpoints and small sample sizes, which are the main limitations of the conducted studies. The findings substantiate the need for further research using endpoints (clinical efficacy, surrogate markers) and post-marketing safety monitoring (primarily for local and systemic AEs).
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease. The central question is to evaluate the efficacy and safety of mRNA vaccines against rabies virus, with a specific emphasis on clinical trials and their outcomes.
Evidence base and method
The authors analyzed publications in PubMed, eLIBRARY.RU, and records in ClinicalTrials.gov describing the results of controlled clinical trials of mRNA vaccines for the prevention of infectious diseases (excluding COVID-19) from 2016 to 2026. The review included studies on RSV infection, CMV infection, seasonal influenza, Zika fever, and rabies. The methodology involved systematic analysis of clinical trial data, with attention to surrogate endpoints and sample size limitations.
Consensus and conclusions
The mRNA vaccine CV7201 against rabies induced protective titers of virus-neutralizing antibodies in 71% of study participants after intradermal administration and in 46% after intramuscular administration. The safety profile was considered favorable, but the incidence of local and systemic adverse events (AEs) was high (97% and 78%, respectively). Clinical efficacy was confirmed only for the mRNA-1345 vaccine against RSV infection, but this is not directly relevant to rabies. The safety profile of mRNA vaccines against rabies was considered satisfactory, though the review notes limitations in the evaluation of efficacy using surrogate endpoints and small sample sizes.
Controversies and gaps
The evaluation of most mRNA vaccines for the prevention of infectious diseases, including rabies, is based on surrogate endpoints and small sample sizes, which are the main limitations of the conducted studies. The review highlights that the use of surrogate endpoints may not fully capture clinical efficacy, and there is insufficient data to detect rare adverse events. Additionally, the review notes that the safety profile of mRNA vaccines against rabies, while considered favorable, is associated with a high incidence of local and systemic adverse events.
Research and practice implications
The findings substantiate the need for further research using clinical efficacy endpoints and surrogate markers. Post-marketing safety monitoring is emphasized, particularly for local and systemic adverse events. The review also suggests the importance of addressing the limitations of surrogate endpoints and small sample sizes in future studies to improve the evaluation of mRNA vaccines for rabies prevention.

加纳 Techiman 大规模狗 疫苗接种 运动的评估。

Evaluation of mass dog vaccination campaign in Techiman, Ghana.
作者： Prince Kyere Dwaah, Sylvia Afriyie Squire, Nana Yaa Awua-Boateng, Denis Dekugmen Yar, David Kando, Abraham Num, Princess Amissah, Helen Djang-Fordjour, Dwaah Prince Kyere
摘要中文翻译背景:在撒哈拉以南非洲地区,大规模狗 疫苗 计划的实施经常受到不一致的 疫苗 采购、融资限制、冷链要求和分销系统弱点的限制,而不是完全缺乏 疫苗。在狗群中实现并维持至少 70% 的 疫苗接种 覆盖率被广泛认为对于中断 传播 至关重要;然而,在快速城市化、资源有限的环境中开展的市政运动的可操作证据有限。我们评估了 2024 年 9 月在加纳泰奇曼南大都会开展的一项由 One Health 协调的免费大众狗 狂犬病 疫苗接种 活动的实施情况和结果。方法:在活动结束两周后进行混合方法的活动后评估。使用结构化调查问卷从 200 个养狗家庭收集了定量数据,并审查了行政 疫苗接种 记录以估计总体覆盖范围。此外,还对参与活动实施的兽医官员、公共卫生专业人员、环境卫生人员和社区领袖进行了关键知情人访谈。使用描述性统计来总结家庭参与和意识指标,同时对定性数据进行专题分析。主要调查结果:在接受调查的家庭中,59.5% 的家庭拥有超过一只狗,51.5% 的家庭表示在活动期间至少为一只狗接种了疫苗。活动结束后,对 狂犬病 致命的认知度从 45%(自我报告的活动前回忆)增加到 75%,而对 狂犬病 可预防的认知度从 40% 增加到 70%。行政记录显示,大约有 5,600 只狗接种了疫苗,根据预测的狗数量数据,相当于大都市覆盖率的 70%。与会者认为强有力的多部门合作和世界 狂犬病 日动员是关键推动因素。实施障碍包括 疫苗 供应延迟、冷链能力有限、城郊社区的通道不足以及缺乏为自由漫游的狗接种疫苗的策略。结论/意义:评估表明,Techiman 大众狗 疫苗接种 运动的行政 疫苗接种 覆盖率超过了国际建议的阈值,同时确定了可能影响有效群体免疫的实施障碍。研究结果强调了改善 疫苗 物流、扩大进入城郊社区的机会、加强监测系统以及在未来的活动中纳入为自由漫步的狗接种疫苗的策略的重要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病,特别评估了加纳 Techiman South Metropolitan 大规模狗 狂犬病 疫苗接种 运动的实施和结果。核心问题是此类活动是否可以在资源有限的环境中有效地中断 狂犬病 传播。
证据基础与方法
活动结束两周后进行了混合方法的活动后评估。使用结构化调查问卷从 200 个养狗家庭收集了定量数据,并审查了行政 疫苗接种 记录以估计总体覆盖范围。使用描述性统计来总结家庭参与和意识指标,同时对定性数据进行专题分析。该研究没有报告荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
该活动实现了约 70% 的 疫苗接种 行政覆盖率,超过了国际建议的阈值。对 狂犬病 致命的认识从 45% 增加到 75%,对 狂犬病 可预防的认识从 40% 增加到 70%。评估表明,该运动在实现覆盖方面有效,但发现了可能影响人群免疫力的实施障碍。
争议与缺口
在快速城市化、资源有限的环境中,市政运动的可操作证据有限。人们注意到了实施障碍,例如 疫苗 供应延迟、冷链能力有限、城郊社区的准入不足以及缺乏为自由漫游的狗接种疫苗的策略。这些因素可能会影响未来活动的有效性。
科研与实践启示
建议在未来的活动中改善 疫苗 物流、扩大城市周边社区的覆盖范围、加强监测系统以及纳入为自由漫游的狗接种疫苗的策略。研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以确保持续的覆盖和有效的人群免疫力。

Evaluation of mass dog vaccination campaign in Techiman, Ghana.

Authors: Prince Kyere Dwaah, Sylvia Afriyie Squire, Nana Yaa Awua-Boateng, Denis Dekugmen Yar, David Kando, Abraham Num, Princess Amissah, Helen Djang-Fordjour, Dwaah Prince Kyere
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Across Sub-Saharan Africa, implementation of mass dog vaccination programmes is frequently constrained by inconsistent vaccine procurement, financing limitations, cold-chain requirements, and weaknesses in distribution systems, rather than complete vaccine unavailability. Achieving and sustaining at least 70% vaccination coverage in dog populations is widely recognized as essential for interrupting transmission; however, there is limited operational evidence from municipal campaigns conducted in rapidly urbanizing, resource-constrained settings. We evaluated the implementation and outcomes of a One Health-coordinated free mass dog rabies vaccination campaign conducted in September 2024 in Techiman South Metropolitan, Ghana. METHODOLOGY: A mixed-methods post-campaign assessment was undertaken two weeks after campaign completion. Quantitative data were collected from 200 dog-owning households using structured questionnaires, and administrative vaccination records were reviewed to estimate overall coverage. In addition, key informant interviews were conducted with veterinary officers, public health professionals, environmental health personnel, and community leaders involved in campaign delivery. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize household participation and awareness indicators, while qualitative data were analyzed thematically. PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: Among surveyed households, 59.5% owned more than one dog and 51.5% reported vaccinating at least one dog during the campaign. Awareness that rabies is fatal increased from 45% (self-reported pre-campaign recall) to 75% after the campaign period, while awareness that rabies is preventable increased from 40% to 70%. Administrative records indicated that approximately 5,600 dogs were vaccinated, corresponding to an estimated 70% metropolitan-level coverage based on projected dog population figures. Participants identified strong multisectoral collaboration and World Rabies Day mobilization as key facilitators. Implementation barriers included vaccine supply delays, limited cold-chain capacity, inadequate access for peri-urban communities, and the absence of strategies to vaccinate free-roaming dogs. CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: The evaluation demonstrated that the Techiman mass dog vaccination campaign achieved administrative vaccination coverage exceeding the internationally recommended threshold while identifying implementation barriers that may influence effective population immunity. The findings highlight the importance of improving vaccine logistics, expanding access to peri-urban communities, strengthening monitoring systems, and incorporating strategies for vaccinating free-roaming dogs during future campaigns.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on the rabies virus and rabies disease, specifically evaluating the implementation and outcomes of a mass dog rabies vaccination campaign in Techiman South Metropolitan, Ghana. The central question is whether such campaigns can effectively interrupt rabies transmission in resource-constrained settings.
Evidence base and method
A mixed-methods post-campaign assessment was conducted two weeks after the campaign completion. Quantitative data were collected from 200 dog-owning households using structured questionnaires, and administrative vaccination records were reviewed to estimate overall coverage. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize household participation and awareness indicators, while qualitative data were analyzed thematically. The study does not report a meta-analysis or consensus process.
Consensus and conclusions
The campaign achieved administrative vaccination coverage of approximately 70%, exceeding the internationally recommended threshold. Awareness that rabies is fatal increased from 45% to 75%, and awareness that rabies is preventable increased from 40% to 70%. The evaluation demonstrated that the campaign was effective in achieving coverage but identified implementation barriers that may affect population immunity.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited operational evidence from municipal campaigns in rapidly urbanizing, resource-constrained settings. Implementation barriers such as vaccine supply delays, limited cold-chain capacity, inadequate access for peri-urban communities, and the absence of strategies to vaccinate free-roaming dogs were noted. These factors may influence the effectiveness of future campaigns.
Research and practice implications
Improving vaccine logistics, expanding access to peri-urban communities, strengthening monitoring systems, and incorporating strategies for vaccinating free-roaming dogs are recommended for future campaigns. The findings emphasize the need for targeted interventions to ensure sustained coverage and effective population immunity.

针对 RNA 病毒的更广谱直接作用 抗病毒药物

Towards broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals against RNA viruses
作者： Hendrik Jan Thibaut, Dirk Jochmans, Christophe Van Dijck, Manon Laporte, Laurens Liesenborghs, Johan Neyts
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。它解决了针对RNA病毒(包括狂犬病病毒)的更广谱直接作用抗病毒药物(BSA)的开发,旨在创建可以针对多个属、科甚至跨科的抗病毒药物。核心问题是这样的广谱抗病毒药物是否可以开发并有效用于狂犬病和其他RNA病毒。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 抗病毒药物 活性抗 RNA 病毒药物(包括 狂犬病病毒)的系统分析。它讨论了开发 BSA 的基本原理,概述了已批准和实验性的 抗病毒药物,并概述了这些药物的生物决定因素和临床开发框架。该综述没有明确报告数据库、检索日期或资格标准,但它是基于对文献的全面审查。
共识与结论
开发更广谱的 抗病毒药物 对于解决当前未满足的医疗需求和加强流行病和大流行的防范至关重要。它强调,狂犬病病毒 与其他 RNA 病毒一样,由于其大流行潜力和广泛的 宿主 范围,是 抗病毒药物 开发的优先事项。该综述还强调了固定剂量组合治疗 RNA 病毒引起的感染的重要性。
争议与缺口
审查指出,一些 抗病毒药物,如阿奇霉素、伊曲康唑和羟氯喹,在临床前证据有限的情况下迅速进行了临床试验,引发了对其有效性和安全性的担忧。此外,审查还强调,“广谱”抗病毒药物 一词缺乏明确的定义,需要更精确的术语和标准。对这些药物 抗病毒药物 谱背后的机制的理解也存在差距。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议研究应集中于开发针对 狂犬病 和其他 RNA 病毒的有效、安全且口服的 抗病毒药物。它还建议加快临床开发框架,特别是暴发,并探索针对RNA病毒感染的治疗的固定剂量组合。此外,它还强调了持续投资 抗病毒药物 药物发现以应对已知和新出现的病毒威胁的重要性。

Towards broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals against RNA viruses

Authors: Hendrik Jan Thibaut, Dirk Jochmans, Christophe Van Dijck, Manon Laporte, Laurens Liesenborghs, Johan Neyts
Original abstract (English)
Nature Reviews Microbiology nature reviews microbiology https://doi.org/10.1038/s41579-026-01372-1 Review article Check for updates Towards broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals against RNA viruses Hendrik Jan Thibaut 1, Dirk Jochmans 1,3, Christophe Van Dijck2,3, Manon Laporte 1,3, Laurens Liesenborghs 2 & Johan Neyts1 Abstract Small molecule antiviral drugs have transformed the management of infections such as those caused by HIV, hepatitis B virus, hepatitis C virus, herpesviruses and influenza viruses. In this Review, we discuss the rationale for building a diversified portfolio of broader-spectrum antivirals against RNA viruses with activity spanning multiple genera, entire families or even crossing viral families. e-mail: johan.neyts@kuleuven.be Nature Reviews Microbiology Review article Alongside vaccine development, sustained investment in antiviral drug discovery is therefore essential. In this Review, we discuss the rationale for the development of BSAs and provide a non-exhaustive overview of approved or experi- mental antiviral drugs active against viruses with an RNA genome. Most of these viruses are RNA viruses — the focus of this Review — including many emerging and clinically important pathogens10–12. Effective antiviral therapeutics remain unavailable for many other often life-threatening viral infections, many of which are caused by RNA viruses. The development of potent, safe and orally available antivirals is therefore essential both to address current unmet medical needs and to strengthen epidemic and pandemic preparedness. We provide a non-exhaustive overview of approved and investigational antiviral agents active against RNA viruses and discuss the biological determinants that underlie their antiviral spectrum. Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease. It addresses the development of broader-spectrum direct-acting antivirals (BSAs) against RNA viruses, including rabies virus, with the aim of creating antivirals that can target multiple genera, families, or even cross families. The central question is whether such broad-spectrum antivirals can be developed and used effectively for rabies and other RNA viruses.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of antiviral agents active against RNA viruses, including rabies virus. It discusses the rationale for developing BSAs, provides an overview of approved and experimental antivirals, and outlines the biological determinants and clinical development frameworks for these drugs. The review does not explicitly report on databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria, but it is based on a comprehensive examination of the literature.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the development of broader-spectrum antivirals is essential to address current unmet medical needs and to strengthen epidemic and pandemic preparedness. It highlights that rabies virus, along with other RNA viruses, is a priority for such antiviral development due to its pandemic potential and broad host range. The review also emphasizes the importance of fixed-dose combinations for treating infections caused by RNA viruses.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that some antivirals, such as azithromycin, itraconazole, and hydroxychloroquine, were rapidly tested in clinical trials with limited preclinical evidence, raising concerns about their efficacy and safety. Additionally, the review highlights that the term 'broad-spectrum' antiviral lacks a clear definition, and there is a need for more precise terminology and criteria. There is also a gap in understanding the mechanisms that underlie the antiviral spectrum of these drugs.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that research should focus on developing potent, safe, and orally available antivirals for rabies and other RNA viruses. It also recommends that clinical development frameworks be accelerated, particularly for outbreaks, and that fixed-dose combinations be explored for the treatment of RNA virus infections. Furthermore, it emphasizes the importance of sustained investment in antiviral drug discovery to address both known and emerging viral threats.

新发人畜共患疾病管理:通过社区视角设计“同一个健康”干预措施,包括心理健康

Emerging zoonotic diseases management: designing One Health interventions through a community lens including mental health
作者： Fatou Ndoye, Marjan Leneman, Jean Augustin Diégane Tine, Abdourahmane Sow, Elhadji Sow, Mouhamadou Wilane, Andree Prisca Ndour, Helena Ngowi, Gilbert Fokou, Benjamin Emikpe
摘要中文翻译简介 One Health (OH) 方法已成为一种认识到健康挑战的复杂性的综合方法。此前发生的埃博拉和 Covid-19 等全球突发卫生事件造成了重大的社会和心理混乱,产生了恐惧、耻辱、不信任、否认疾病和抵制控制措施。本研究旨在调查利益相关者的 OH 观点,并通过社区视角协助设计干预措施,包括心理健康干预措施。方法 这项混合方法研究借鉴了塞内加尔 OH 协调活动和现场经验,于 2022 年至 2024 年之间进行。数据来源包括通过国家 OH 平台实施的活动记录、与达喀尔巴斯德研究所和卫生与社会行动部合作开展的流行病学调查以及 2024 年进行的后续实地调查。通过参与性观察和八个 OH 协调的现场报告收集了定性信息会议以及流行病学调查期间进行的九次半结构化访谈。此外,还对参与流行病防范和应对的政府、机构和社区利益相关者进行了 35 次关键知情人访谈。我们收集了 27 名与 Covid-19、狂犬病 和克里米亚刚果出血热 (CCHF) 相关的患者、幸存者和护理人员以及四名经历过动物损失的牲畜饲养者的叙述。通过对 434 名牲畜饲养者进行调查,评估他们对人畜共患疾病和职业健康干预的知识、看法和做法,获得了定量数据。结果 该研究确定了受影响个人和社区心理困扰的多项指标。对OH协调平台的分析表明,应急响应主要围绕人类、动物和环境卫生部门之间的合作进行组织,业务活动主要集中在监测、病例检测和接触者追踪等方面。然而,社会科学家、心理学家和社会工作者很少参与准备工作和 暴发 应对工作。研究还显示,牲畜饲养者对人畜共患疾病的了解有限,大多数人(89.86%)只认识一两种疾病。知识水平低与正规教育水平较低显着相关(p < 0.03)。然而,受访者展示了当地对这些疾病的了解,并使用当地名称描述了其中一些疾病。大多数受访者 (87.1%) 表示事先不了解 OH 方法,而 75.81% 的受访者知道疾病可以从动物传播给人类。结论 该研究表明,卫生干预措施的设计应通过整合当地知识、加强跨部门合作以及将心理社会维度纳入流行病防范和应对策略,在中央决策、业务规划和社区现实之间架起桥梁。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病,旨在调查利益相关者 One Health 的观点并通过社区视角设计干预措施,包括心理健康方面。
证据基础与方法
这项混合方法研究借鉴了 2022 年至 2024 年期间在塞内加尔开展的“同一个健康”协调活动和实地经验。数据来源包括国家 OH 平台的活动记录、达喀尔巴斯德研究所和卫生与社会行动部的流行病学调查以及 2024 年的实地调查。定性数据是通过参与性观察、八次 OH 协调会议的现场报告以及九次半结构化访谈收集的。通过对 434 名牲畜饲养者进行调查,评估他们对人畜共患疾病和职业健康干预的知识、看法和做法,获得了定量数据。
共识与结论
该研究确定了受影响个人和社区的社会心理困扰指标。它还揭示了牲畜饲养者对人畜共患疾病的了解有限,大多数人只认识一两种疾病。知识水平低与正规教育水平较低显着相关。受访者展示了对这些疾病的当地了解,并使用当地名称描述它们。大多数受访者事先不了解 OH 方法,但许多人都知道动物传染人类的疾病 传播。该研究的结论是,卫生干预措施应整合当地知识,加强跨部门合作,并将社会心理层面纳入流行病防范和应对策略。
争议与缺口
研究指出,社会科学家、心理学家和社会工作者很少参与准备和 暴发 应对工作。对于如何有效地将心理健康考虑因素纳入职业健康干预措施中,人们的理解存在差距。证据还强调需要在普通人群中更好地传播有关人畜共患疾病和卫生保健策略的知识。
科研与实践启示
该研究表明,需要制定更具包容性的职业健康策略,让心理健康专业人员和社区利益相关者参与进来。它强调根据当地知识和实践制定干预措施以及改进人畜共患疾病和职业健康方法教育的重要性。此外,它强调需要进一步研究心理社会因素在 暴发 反应中的作用,并为牲畜饲养者制定有针对性的教育计划。

Emerging zoonotic diseases management: designing One Health interventions through a community lens including mental health

Authors: Fatou Ndoye, Marjan Leneman, Jean Augustin Diégane Tine, Abdourahmane Sow, Elhadji Sow, Mouhamadou Wilane, Andree Prisca Ndour, Helena Ngowi, Gilbert Fokou, Benjamin Emikpe
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The One Health (OH) approach has emerged as a comprehensive approach that recognizes the complexity of health challenges. Previous global health emergencies including Ebola and Covid-19 caused major social and psychological disruptions, generating fear, stigma, distrust, denial of disease and resistance to control measures. This study aims to investigate stakeholder OH perspectives and to assist in designing adaptations for interventions through a community lens, including for mental health. Methods This mixed-methods study draws upon OH coordination activities and field experiences in Senegal and has been conducted between 2022 and 2024. Data sources include records of activities implemented through the National OH platform, epidemiological investigations carried out in collaboration with the Institut Pasteur de Dakar and the Ministry of Health and Social Action, and subsequent field investigations conducted in 2024. Qualitative information has been collected through participatory observation, field reports from eight OH coordination meetings, and nine semi-structured interviews conducted during the epidemiological investigations. In addition, 35 key informant interviews have been conducted with governmental, institutional, and community stakeholders involved in epidemic preparedness and response. Narrative accounts have been collected from 27 patients, survivors, and caregivers in relation to Covid-19, rabies, and Crimean Congo Hemorrhagic Fever (CCHF), as well as from four livestock keepers who had experienced animal losses. Quantitative data have been obtained through a survey of 434 livestock keepers assessing their knowledge, perceptions, and practices related to zoonotic diseases and OH interventions. Results The study has identified several indicators of psychosocial distress among affected individuals and communities. Analysis of the OH coordination platform has shown that emergency responses were primarily organized around collaboration between the human, animal, and environmental health sectors, with operational activities mainly focused on surveillance, case detection, and contact tracing. Yet, social scientists, psychologists, and social workers were rarely involved in preparedness and outbreak responses. The study also revealed limited knowledge of zoonotic diseases among livestock keepers, with the majority (89.86%) recognizing only one or two diseases. Low knowledge was significantly associated with lower levels of formal education ( p < 0.03). However, respondents demonstrated local knowledge of these diseases, describing some of them using local names. Most respondents (87.1%) reported no prior knowledge of the OH approach, while 75.81% were aware that diseases could be transmitted from animals to humans. Conclusion The study suggests that OH interventions should be designed to bridge central decision making, operational planning, and community realities by integrating local knowledge, strengthening cross-sectoral collaboration and incorporating psychosocial dimensions into epidemic preparedness and response strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease, aiming to investigate stakeholder One Health perspectives and design interventions through a community lens, including mental health aspects.
Evidence base and method
This mixed-methods study draws upon One Health coordination activities and field experiences in Senegal, conducted between 2022 and 2024. Data sources include records of activities from the National OH platform, epidemiological investigations with the Institut Pasteur de Dakar and the Ministry of Health and Social Action, and field investigations in 2024. Qualitative data were collected through participatory observation, field reports from eight OH coordination meetings, and nine semi-structured interviews. Quantitative data were obtained through a survey of 434 livestock keepers assessing their knowledge, perceptions, and practices related to zoonotic diseases and OH interventions.
Consensus and conclusions
The study identified psychosocial distress indicators among affected individuals and communities. It also revealed limited knowledge of zoonotic diseases among livestock keepers, with most recognizing only one or two diseases. Low knowledge was significantly associated with lower formal education levels. Respondents showed local knowledge of these diseases, describing them using local names. Most respondents had no prior knowledge of the OH approach, but many were aware of animal-to-human disease transmission. The study concludes that OH interventions should integrate local knowledge, strengthen cross-sectoral collaboration, and incorporate psychosocial dimensions into epidemic preparedness and response strategies.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that social scientists, psychologists, and social workers were rarely involved in preparedness and outbreak responses. There is a gap in understanding how to effectively integrate mental health considerations into OH interventions. The evidence also highlights a need for better knowledge dissemination about zoonotic diseases and OH strategies among the general population.
Research and practice implications
The study suggests the need for more inclusive OH strategies that involve mental health professionals and community stakeholders. It emphasizes the importance of tailoring interventions to local knowledge and practices, and improving education on zoonotic diseases and OH approaches. Additionally, it highlights the need for further research on the role of psychosocial factors in outbreak responses and the development of targeted educational programs for livestock keepers.

社论:测量 疫苗接种 认知、信心和吸收:推进仪器开发和验证

Editorial: Measuring vaccination perception, confidence, and uptake: advancing instrument development and validation
作者： Divya S. Subramaniam, Aida Bianco
摘要中文翻译本研究主题中的两项贡献直接推进了推动本主题的测量科学。为在 3C 框架中制定针对美国成年人的成人 疫苗 犹豫的新通用衡量标准奠定基础,报告内容和表面有效性调查结果,包括来自专家小组审查的内容有效性指数,旨在捕获跨 疫苗 类型而不是单一指标的犹豫。报告针对 COVID-19 疫苗接种 (VHAPS-CV19) 的 疫苗 犹豫和感知量表 (VHAPS-CV19) 的开发和心理测量评估,采用带有最大方差旋转的主轴因子分解来导出双因素结构,区分犹豫因素(信心、自满和便利)与感知和信任因素(感知利益、障碍、敏感性、严重性和信任),以及每个分量表的内部一致性估计。这两项研究共同追踪了有效测量这种复杂结构所需的各个阶段,从项目生成和内容有效性到因子结构和可靠性测试,并为该领域留下了两种补充工具,一种是通用的,一种是 疫苗 专用的,用于未来的验证和跨文化适应工作。本主题中的一些贡献考察了医疗保健工作者的犹豫和接受情况,他们自己的 疫苗 行为和患者沟通直接塑造了公众的信心。采用横断面设计调查了中国医护人员对季节性流感 疫苗接种 的吸收、意愿和实践。同样,他们采用了一项横断面调查来探讨新加坡国家传染病中心医护人员对 COVID-19 疫苗接种 的态度,而 Aksoy 等人。对土耳其的社区药剂师进行了调查,了解他们对 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和障碍,并将重点扩大到医院以外的一线倡导者。采用独特的定性方法,采访了伦敦一家医院的医护人员,以确定麻疹 疫苗接种 吸收的障碍和促进因素;这一设计补充了该主题其他地方的定量工作,揭示了结构化调查往往会忽略的工作场所、信息和人际因素。第二组贡献解决了社区和一般人群样本中的犹豫和吸收决定因素,将该主题的范围扩展到流感和 COVID-19 之外,扩展到其他 疫苗 类型和生命阶段。阿里等人完善了该系列。对海湾合作委员会国家医护人员中流感 疫苗 摄取的促进因素和障碍进行叙述性回顾,综合来自相对于北美、欧洲和东亚的 疫苗 犹豫文献中代表性相对不足的区域背景的证据。贡献了独特但互补的观点,使用真实世界数据对 狂犬病 疫苗接种 后不良事件的风险因素进行了多变量分析。这项工作的重点是 疫苗 的安全性而不是感知,但它提醒人们,对 疫苗 的信心不仅取决于态度和可及性,还取决于生活经验的耐受性,并且以感知为中心的工具最好与药物警戒数据一起阅读。该集合中有一些突出的模式(图 1;表 1)。从地域上看,贡献集中在中国(六项研究),其他代表来自美国、新加坡、土耳其、越南、英国和海湾合作委员会地区;撒哈拉以南非洲、南亚和拉丁美洲的人口不存在,这限制了这些发现的推广程度,并为未来的研究主题指明了方向。从方法上来说,大多数贡献都依赖于横截面的自我报告调查。这些设计对于生成有关犹豫相关性的假设很有用,但它们无法在态度和行为之间建立时间优先级,并且它们仍然容易受到社会期望和回忆偏差的影响。只有一篇文章采用了定性访谈方法,没有一篇文章采用了纵向或实验设计;这种集中的设计反映了更广泛的犹豫文献,并标志着未来工作应该弥合的差距。总而言之,对该研究主题的贡献清楚地表明,疫苗 决策具有多个层次。犹豫和信心是由个人心理、专业角色、文化背景、服务可及性以及对 疫苗 安全的直接体验决定的。仪器开发研究回应了真正的方法论差距:该领域需要心理测量上合理且适用于不同人群的测量工具。决定因素研究提出了相反的背景情况。海湾地区医护人员、中国西部老年人和土耳其药剂师的研究结果不可互换;每个都提供了对所研究环境的见解,而不是对 疫苗 犹豫的普遍解释。未来的研究仍然存在一些空白。这里介绍的工具的跨文化验证,包括测量不变性测试以及与 WHO SAGE 疫苗 犹豫量表 (2) 等基准的比较,将增强对其在人群中适用性的信心。纵向设计跟踪测量的犹豫或信心如何与实际的 疫苗接种 行为随时间的变化而不仅仅是意图相关,将有助于缩小态度测量和吸收结果之间有据可查的差距。更多地使用定性和混合方法设计,以及这里占主导地位的横截面调查,将有助于澄清定量模型识别但无法完全解释的机制。最后,随着 疫苗 平台和通信环境的发展,测量工具将需要定期重新验证,以保持对新出现的犹豫决定因素的敏感度。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。它研究了 疫苗 感知、信心和吸收的测量,特别是在医护人员和普通人群中,重点是开发心理测量学上合理的工具来评估不同背景下的 疫苗 犹豫度。
证据基础与方法
该评论是一篇社论,汇集了各种研究的贡献。这些研究主要使用横断面、自我报告调查来调查 疫苗 的犹豫和接受情况。一些研究采用定性访谈方法,但没有使用纵向或实验设计。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程,而是综合了多项研究的结果。
共识与结论
审查强调,疫苗 决策涉及多种因素,包括个人心理、专业角色、文化背景、服务可及性以及对 疫苗 安全的直接体验。它强调需要心理测量上合理且适用于不同人群的测量工具,以及跨文化验证和与 WHO SAGE 疫苗 犹豫量表等既定量表进行比较的重要性。
争议与缺口
该评论指出了方法上的局限性,例如横断面设计无法在态度和行为之间建立时间优先级,以及它们容易受到社会期望和回忆偏差的影响。它还指出,不同人群(例如海湾地区的医护人员、中国西部的老年人和土耳其的药剂师)的研究结果不可互换,表明缺乏普遍性。此外,审查还指出,撒哈拉以南非洲、南亚和拉丁美洲缺乏研究,这限制了研究结果的范围。
科研与实践启示
审查建议未来的研究应侧重于所引入工具的跨文化验证,包括测量不变性测试和针对现有量表的基准测试。它还建议使用纵向设计来更好地了解测量的犹豫或置信度与实际 疫苗接种 行为随时间的关系。此外,它还强调了随着 疫苗 平台和通信环境的发展,需要定期重新验证测量工具。

Editorial: Measuring vaccination perception, confidence, and uptake: advancing instrument development and validation

Authors: Divya S. Subramaniam, Aida Bianco
Original abstract (English)
Two contributions in this Research Topic directly advance the measurement science that motivated this Topic. ground the development of a new general measure of adult vaccine hesitancy for U.S. adults in the 3C framework, reporting content and face validity findings, including a content validity index derived from expert panel review, for an instrument intended to capture hesitancy across vaccine types rather than a single indication. report the development and psychometric evaluation of the Vaccine Hesitancy and Perception Scale toward COVID-19 vaccination (VHAPS-CV19), employing principal axis factoring with varimax rotation to derive a two-factor structure that distinguishes a hesitancy factor (confidence, complacency, and convenience) from a perception-and-trust factor (perceived benefit, barrier, susceptibility, severity, and trust), together with internal consistency estimates for each subscale. Together, the two studies trace the stages that valid measurement of a construct this complex demands, from item generation and content validity through factor structure and reliability testing, and they leave the field with two complementary tools, one general-purpose and one vaccine-specific, for future validation and cross-cultural adaptation work.Several contributions in this Topic examine hesitancy and uptake among healthcare workers, a population whose own vaccination behavior and patient communication directly shape public confidence. investigated uptake, willingness, and practices toward seasonal influenza vaccination among healthcare workers in China using a cross-sectional design. similarly employed a cross-sectional survey to explore attitudes toward COVID-19 vaccination among healthcare workers at a national infectious disease center in Singapore, while Aksoy et al. surveyed community pharmacists in Türkiye regarding their knowledge, attitudes, and barriers toward vaccination, extending the focus to frontline advocates outside the hospital setting. took a distinct, qualitative approach, interviewing healthcare workers at a London hospital to identify barriers and facilitators to measles vaccination uptake; this design complements the quantitative work elsewhere in the Topic, surfacing workplace, informational, and interpersonal factors that structured surveys tend to miss.A second cluster of contributions addresses hesitancy and uptake determinants in community and general-population samples, extending the scope of this Topic beyond influenza and COVID-19 to additional vaccine types and life stages. Rounding out the collection, Ali et al. provide a narrative review of enablers and barriers to influenza vaccine uptake among healthcare workers in Gulf Cooperation Council countries, synthesizing evidence from a regional context that is comparatively underrepresented in the vaccine hesitancy literature relative to North America, Europe, and East Asia. contribute a distinct but complementary perspective, presenting a multivariate analysis of risk factors for adverse events following rabies vaccination using real-world data. This work centers on vaccine safety rather than perception, but it is a reminder that confidence in vaccination rests on lived experience with tolerability as much as on attitudes and access, and that perception-focused instruments are best read alongside pharmacovigilance data.A few patterns stand out across this collection (Figure 1; Table 1). Geographically, contributions concentrate in China (six studies), with additional representation from the United States, Singapore, Türkiye, Vietnam, the United Kingdom, and the Gulf Cooperation Council region; populations in Sub-Saharan Africa, South Asia, and Latin America are absent, which limits how far these findings generalize and points to a direction for future Research Topics. Methodologically, most contributions rely on cross-sectional, self-report surveys. These designs are useful for generating hypotheses about correlates of hesitancy, but they cannot establish temporal precedence between attitude and behavior, and they remain vulnerable to social desirability and recall bias. Only one contribution employed qualitative interview methods, and none employed a longitudinal or experimental design; this concentration of designs mirrors the broader hesitancy literature and marks a gap that future work should close.Taken together, the contributions to this Research Topic make clear that vaccine decision-making has many layers. Hesitancy and confidence are shaped at once by individual psychology, professional role, cultural context, service accessibility, and direct experience with vaccine safety. The instrument development studies respond to a real methodological gap: the field needs measurement tools that are psychometrically sound and adaptable across populations. The determinant studies argue the opposite case for context. Findings from healthcare workers in the Gulf region, older adults in western China, and pharmacists in Türkiye are not interchangeable; each offers insight for the setting studied rather than a universal account of vaccine hesitancy.A few gaps remain for future research. Cross-cultural validation of the instruments introduced here, including measurement invariance testing and comparison against benchmarks such as the WHO SAGE Vaccine Hesitancy Scale (2), would strengthen confidence in their applicability across populations. Longitudinal designs tracking how measured hesitancy or confidence relates to actual vaccination behavior over time, rather than to intention alone, would help close the well-documented gap between attitudinal measurement and uptake outcomes. Greater use of qualitative and mixed-methods designs, alongside the cross-sectional surveys predominant here, would help clarify mechanisms that quantitative models identify but cannot fully explain. Finally, as vaccine platforms and communication environments evolve, measurement tools will need periodic re-validation to remain sensitive to emerging determinants of hesitancy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease. It examines the measurement of vaccination perception, confidence, and uptake, particularly among healthcare workers and general populations, with an emphasis on developing psychometrically sound instruments for assessing vaccine hesitancy across different contexts.
Evidence base and method
The review is an editorial that compiles contributions from various studies. These studies primarily use cross-sectional, self-report surveys to investigate vaccine hesitancy and uptake. A few studies employ qualitative interview methods, while none use longitudinal or experimental designs. The review does not explicitly report a meta-analysis or consensus process but synthesizes findings from multiple studies.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that vaccine decision-making involves multiple factors, including individual psychology, professional role, cultural context, service accessibility, and direct experience with vaccine safety. It emphasizes the need for measurement tools that are psychometrically sound and adaptable across populations, as well as the importance of cross-cultural validation and comparison against established scales like the WHO SAGE Vaccine Hesitancy Scale.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies methodological limitations, such as the inability of cross-sectional designs to establish temporal precedence between attitude and behavior, and their vulnerability to social desirability and recall bias. It also notes that findings from different populations (e.g., healthcare workers in the Gulf region, older adults in western China, and pharmacists in Türkiye) are not interchangeable, indicating a lack of generalizability. Additionally, the review points out the absence of studies in Sub-Saharan Africa, South Asia, and Latin America, which limits the scope of the findings.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on cross-cultural validation of the instruments introduced, including measurement invariance testing and benchmarking against existing scales. It also recommends the use of longitudinal designs to better understand how measured hesitancy or confidence relates to actual vaccination behavior over time. Furthermore, it highlights the need for periodic re-validation of measurement tools as vaccine platforms and communication environments evolve.

吉尔福德县确认 2026 年第十四起动物 狂犬病 病例 - 吉尔福德县政府 (.gov)

Guilford County Confirms Fourteenth Case of Animal Rabies in 2026 - Guilford County Government (.gov)
新闻简报2026年10月8日,北卡罗来纳州吉尔福德县确认了2026年第14例动物狂犬病病例。詹姆斯敦卡恩街的一只浣熊狂犬病检测呈阳性。县卫生机构正在向居民发出有关确诊病例的警报,并强调 狂犬病 预防的重要性。该事件已得到确认,风险涉及人类和动物的潜在暴露。建议居民避免接触野生动物,在户外监督宠物,并向动物控制部门报告任何流浪动物。提供联系方式以获取更多信息和帮助。应对措施包括提高公众认识活动和预防措施,以减轻 狂犬病 的传播。
查看新闻五要素
时间
吉尔福德县于2026年10月8日确认了2026年第14例动物狂犬病病例。事件日期为2026年10月8日。
地点与对象
该案件发生在吉尔福德县,特别是詹姆斯敦的卡恩街。受影响的人群包括吉尔福德县的居民和该地区的动物。
事件
一只浣熊的 狂犬病病毒 检测结果呈阳性。吉尔福德县卫生与公众服务局公共卫生部报告了这一确诊病例。该事件已确认。
规模、影响与风险
这是 2026 年第 14 例确诊的动物 狂犬病 病例。风险被明确表述为 狂犬病 接触人类和动物的可能性,但没有提供具体病例或死亡人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
吉尔福德县动物服务处和健康与人类服务局正在提供有关 狂犬病 预防的信息。联系方式可供进一步查询。该状况正在调查中,需要公众提高认识并采取预防措施。

Guilford County Confirms Fourteenth Case of Animal Rabies in 2026 - Guilford County Government (.gov)

News Brief
Guilford County, North Carolina, confirmed the fourteenth case of animal rabies in 2026 on October 8, 2026. A raccoon on Kearn Street in Jamestown tested positive for the rabies virus. The county health agency is alerting residents about the confirmed case and emphasizing the importance of rabies prevention. The event is confirmed, and the risk involves potential exposure to humans and animals. Residents are advised to avoid contact with wildlife, supervise pets outdoors, and report any stray animals to Animal Control. Contact details are provided for further information and assistance. The response includes public awareness campaigns and preventive measures to mitigate the spread of rabies.

This is the fourteenth confirmed case of animal rabies in 2026. North Carolina law requires that all domestic pets (cats, dogs and ferrets), whether living inside or outside, aged four months or older be vaccinated for rabies. Animals that are confined in outdoor fenced areas should also have current rabies vaccinations. Supervising pets when they are outdoors could prevent contact with wild animals and lessen the possibility of rabies exposure. Guilford County Animal Services may be contacted for more information on rabies prevention at 336-641-2506 or visit our Animal Services website. For information regarding the current rabies cases, please contact Jorge Ortega at 336-641-3409 or jortega1@guilfordcountync.gov. Rabies circulates within our wildlife population throughout the entire year. Here are some tips to help prevent you or your family from being exposed to the rabies virus: Avoid direct contact with wildlife, dead or alive. Do not approach, try to play with, touch, rescue or treat any wildlife. If you find a sick or injured animal, contact Animal Control at 336-641-5990. Avoid any animal displaying unnatural behavior. Wild animals are not usually friendly so be very careful if they approach you. Do not try to separate fighting animals. Feed your pets indoors. Leaving food outside will attract dogs, cats and other wildlife into your yard. Report all stray animals to Animal Control at 336-641-5990 in Greensboro and 336-883-3224 in High Point. Stray animals may not have current rabies vaccinations. If a person is bitten by an animal (domestic or stray), wash the bitten area immediately with soap and water, seek medical attention, and report the bite to local Animal Control Officers.
